00090: function emptyAnalytics() {
00091:   return { top: [], meta: null };
00092: }

00094: function drawTs(draw) {
00095:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
00096:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
00097: 
00098:   if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
00099: 
00100:   const [Y, M, D] = String(y).split("-").map(Number);
00101:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
00102: 
00103:   if (!Number.isFinite(mins) || mins < 0) {
00104:     return Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
00105:   }
00106: 
00107:   return Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D) + mins * 60 * 1000;
00108: }
00109: 
00110: function drawKey(draw) {
00111:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
00112:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
00113:   return isYMD(y) && h ? `${y}|${h}` : "";
00114: }
00115: 
00116: function hasDrawAtHour(draws, hourBucket) {
00117:   const target = toHourBucket(hourBucket);
00118:   if (!target) return false;
00119: 
00120:   return (Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []).some((d) => {
00121:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
00122:     return h === target;
00123:   });
00124: }
00125: 
00126: function mergeBaseIntoRange(rangeDraws, baseDraw) {
00127:   const list = Array.isArray(rangeDraws) ? rangeDraws : [];
00128:   const key = drawKey(baseDraw);
00129: 
00130:   if (!key) return list;
00131: 
00132:   const map = new Map();
00133: 
00134:   for (const d of list) {
00135:     const k = drawKey(d);
00136:     if (k) map.set(k, d);
00137:   }
00138: 
00139:   if (!map.has(key)) {
00140:     map.set(key, baseDraw);
00141:   }
00142: 
00143:   return Array.from(map.values()).sort((a, b) => drawTs(a) - drawTs(b));
00144: }
00145: 
00146: function isDrawValidForLotterySchedule(draw, lotteryKey) {
00147:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
00148:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
00149: 
00150:   if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return false;
00151: 
00152:   const schedule = getScheduleForLottery({
00153:     lotteryKey,
00154:     ymd: y,
00155:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00156:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
00157:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
00158:   });
00159: 
00160:   const normalizedSchedule = Array.isArray(schedule)
00161:     ? schedule.map(toHourBucket).filter(Boolean)
00162:     : [];
00163: 
00164:   return normalizedSchedule.includes(h);
00165: }

00167: function findLatestHistoricalBaseDraw({
00168:   draws,
00169:   lotteryKey,
00170:   targetYmd,
00171:   targetHourBucket,
00172: }) {
00173:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
00174:   const targetHour = toHourBucket(targetHourBucket);
00175: 
00176:   if (!isYMD(targetYmd) || !targetHour) {
00177:     return { draw: null, ymd: "", hour: "", source: "none" };
00178:   }
00179: 
00180:   const [Y, M, D] = String(targetYmd).split("-").map(Number);
00181:   const mins = hourToInt(targetHour);
00182:   const targetTs = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D) + mins * 60 * 1000;
00183: 
00184:   let best = null;
00185:   let bestTs = Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
00186: 
00187:   for (const d of list) {
00188:     if (!isDrawValidForLotterySchedule(d, lotteryKey)) continue;
00189: 
00190:     const ts = drawTs(d);
00191:     if (!Number.isFinite(ts)) continue;
00192:     if (ts >= targetTs) continue;
00193: 
00194:     if (ts > bestTs) {
00195:       best = d;
00196:       bestTs = ts;
00197:     }
00198:   }
00199: 
00200:   if (!best) {
00201:     return { draw: null, ymd: "", hour: "", source: "none" };
00202:   }
00203: 
00204:   return {
00205:     draw: best,
00206:     ymd: pickDrawYMD(best) || "",
00207:     hour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(best)) || "",
00208:     source: "history_range",
00209:   };
00210: }
00211: 
00212: function sanitizeHistoricalDraws({ draws, lotteryKey, baseDraw }) {
00213:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
00214:   const baseTs = drawTs(baseDraw);
00215: 
00216:   if (!baseDraw || !Number.isFinite(baseTs)) return [];
00217: 
00218:   return list
00219:     .filter((d) => isDrawValidForLotterySchedule(d, lotteryKey))
00220:     .filter((d) => {
00221:       const ts = drawTs(d);
00222:       return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts <= baseTs;
00223:     })
00224:     .sort((a, b) => drawTs(a) - drawTs(b));
00225: }

00227: function parseTargetDate(ymd, hour) {
00228:   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return null;
00229: 
00230:   const h = toHourBucket(hour);
00231:   const m = String(h || "").match(/^(\d{2})h$/);
00232: 
00233:   if (!m) return null;
00234: 
00235:   const [Y, M, D] = String(ymd).split("-").map(Number);
00236:   const hh = Number(m[1]);
00237: 
00238:   const dt = new Date(Y, M - 1, D, hh, 0, 0, 0);
00239:   return Number.isNaN(dt.getTime()) ? null : dt;
00240: }
00241: 
00242: function isFutureTarget(ymd, hour) {
00243:   const target = parseTargetDate(ymd, hour);
00244:   if (!target) return false;
00245:   return target.getTime() > Date.now();
00246: }

00277:         Array.isArray(item?.imgIcon) && item.imgIcon.length
00278:           ? item.imgIcon.filter(Boolean)

00281:       return {
00282:         ...item,

00286:           safeStr(item?.animal) ||
00287:           safeStr(getAnimalLabel(grupo)),

00299:       };

00321: function debugTop3Effect(name, details = {}) {
00322:   if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
00323: 
00324:   const enabled =
00325:     new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get(
00326:       "debugTop3Effects"
00327:     ) === "1";
00328: 
00329:   if (!enabled) return;
00330: 
00331:   const counters =
00332:     window.__TOP3_EFFECT_COUNTS__ ||
00333:     (window.__TOP3_EFFECT_COUNTS__ = {});
00334: 
00335:   counters[name] = Number(counters[name] || 0) + 1;
00336: 
00337:   console.info(
00338:     "[TOP3 EFFECT]",
00339:     name,
00340:     `#${counters[name]}`,
00341:     details
00342:   );
00343: }

00001: import { safeStr, isYMD, toHourBucket } from "./top3.formatters";
00002: 
00003: import {
00004:   pickDrawYMD,
00005:   pickDrawHour,
00006:   pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw,
00007:   getScheduleForLottery,
00008: } from "./top3.engine";
00009: 
00010: import {
00011:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00012:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
00013:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
00014: } from "./top3.constants";
00015: 
00016: // ===============================
00017: // TOP3 LOG SYSTEM (VALIDAÇÃO REAL)
00018: // ===============================
00019: 
00020: function normHour(h) {
00021:   return toHourBucket(h) || "";
00022: }
00023: 
00024: function makeKey(ymd, hour) {
00025:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
00026:   const h = normHour(hour);
00027:   return isYMD(y) && h ? `${y}_${h}` : "";
00028: }
00029: 
00030: function parseTargetDate(ymd, hour) {
00031:   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return null;
00032: 
00033:   const hourBucket = normHour(hour);
00034:   const m = String(hourBucket || "").match(/^(\d{2})h$/);
00035:   if (!m) return null;
00036: 
00037:   const [Y, M, D] = String(ymd).split("-").map(Number);
00038:   const hh = Number(m[1]);
00039: 
00040:   const dt = new Date(Y, M - 1, D, hh, 0, 0, 0);
00041:   return Number.isNaN(dt.getTime()) ? null : dt;
00042: }
00043: 
00044: function isFutureTarget(ymd, hour) {
00045:   const target = parseTargetDate(ymd, hour);
00046:   if (!target) return false;
00047:   return target.getTime() > Date.now();
00048: }
00049: 
00050: function resolvePendingStatus(ymd, hour, picks) {
00051:   const hasPicks = Array.isArray(picks) && picks.length > 0;
00052:   if (!hasPicks) return "empty";
00053:   return isFutureTarget(ymd, hour) ? "future" : "pending_result";
00054: }
00055: 
00056: function normalizePicks(picks) {
00057:   return Array.from(
00058:     new Set(
00059:       (Array.isArray(picks) ? picks : [])
00060:         .map(Number)
00061:         .filter((n) => Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 1 && n <= 25)
00062:     )
00063:   ).slice(0, 3);
00064: }
00065: 
00066: function normalizeResult(resultValue) {
00067:   const n = Number(resultValue);
00068:   return Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 1 && n <= 25 ? n : null;
00069: }
00070: 
00071: function normalizeLogEntry(entry) {
00072:   if (!entry || typeof entry !== "object") return null;
00073: 
00074:   const ymd = safeStr(entry?.target?.ymd || "");
00075:   const hour = normHour(entry?.target?.hour || "");
00076:   const targetKey = makeKey(ymd, hour);
00077: 
00078:   if (!targetKey) return null;
00079: 
00080:   const picks = normalizePicks(entry?.picks);
00081:   const result = normalizeResult(entry?.result);
00082: 
00083:   const hit =
00084:     result != null && picks.length
00085:       ? picks.includes(result)
00086:       : typeof entry?.hit === "boolean"
00087:         ? entry.hit
00088:         : null;
00089: 
00090:   const status =
00091:     result != null ? "validated" : resolvePendingStatus(ymd, hour, picks);
00092: 
00093:   return {
00094:     ...entry,
00095:     targetKey,
00096:     target: { ymd, hour },
00097:     picks,
00098:     result,
00099:     hit,
00100:     createdAt: Number(entry?.createdAt) || Date.now(),
00101:     validatedAt:
00102:       result != null && Number(entry?.validatedAt)
00103:         ? Number(entry.validatedAt)
00104:         : undefined,
00105:     status,
00106:   };
00107: }
00108: 
00109: function scoreLogEntry(entry) {
00110:   if (!entry) return -1;
00111: 
00112:   let score = 0;
00113:   if (Array.isArray(entry?.picks) && entry.picks.length) score += 20;
00114:   if (entry?.result != null) score += 10;
00115:   if (typeof entry?.hit === "boolean") score += 5;
00116:   if (entry?.validatedAt) score += 2;
00117:   if (entry?.createdAt) score += 1;
00118: 
00119:   return score;
00120: }
00121: 
00122: function normalizeAndDedupeLog(log) {
00123:   const list = Array.isArray(log) ? log : [];
00124:   const byKey = new Map();
00125: 
00126:   for (const raw of list) {
00127:     const entry = normalizeLogEntry(raw);
00128:     if (!entry) continue;
00129: 
00130:     const prev = byKey.get(entry.targetKey);
00131: 
00132:     if (!prev || scoreLogEntry(entry) >= scoreLogEntry(prev)) {
00133:       byKey.set(entry.targetKey, entry);
00134:     }
00135:   }
00136: 
00137:   return [...byKey.values()].sort((a, b) => {
00138:     const aKey = `${a?.target?.ymd || ""}_${a?.target?.hour || ""}`;
00139:     const bKey = `${b?.target?.ymd || ""}_${b?.target?.hour || ""}`;
00140:     return aKey < bKey ? -1 : aKey > bKey ? 1 : 0;
00141:   });
00142: }
00143: 
00144: function getTop3Log() {
00145:   try {
00146:     const raw = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("top3_log") || "[]");
00147:     return normalizeAndDedupeLog(raw);
00148:   } catch {
00149:     return [];
00150:   }
00151: }
00152: 
00153: function saveTop3Log(log) {
00154:   try {
00155:     const normalized = normalizeAndDedupeLog(log).slice(-200);
00156:     localStorage.setItem("top3_log", JSON.stringify(normalized));
00157:   } catch {}
00158: }
00159: 
00160: function registerPrediction({
00161:   targetKey,
00162:   targetYmd,
00163:   targetHour,
00164:   picks,
00165:   snapshot = [],
00166:   engineVersion = "",
00167: }) {
00168:   const ymd = safeStr(targetYmd);
00169:   const hour = normHour(targetHour);
00170:   const normalizedKey = targetKey || makeKey(ymd, hour);
00171: 
00172:   if (!isYMD(ymd) || !hour || !normalizedKey) return;
00173: 
00174:   const normalizedPicks = normalizePicks(picks);
00175:   if (!normalizedPicks.length) return;
00176: 
00177:   const log = getTop3Log();
00178: 
00179:   const idx = log.findIndex(
00180:     (l) => String(l?.targetKey || "") === String(normalizedKey)
00181:   );
00182: 
00183:   const status = resolvePendingStatus(ymd, hour, normalizedPicks);
00184: 
00185:   if (idx >= 0) {
00186:     const prev = log[idx] || {};
00187: 
00188:     if (prev.result != null || prev.status === "validated") return;
00189: 
00190:     log[idx] = {
00191:       ...prev,
00192:       targetKey: normalizedKey,
00193:       target: { ymd, hour },
00194:       picks: normalizedPicks,
00195:       result: null,
00196:       hit: null,
00197:       snapshot:
00198:         Array.isArray(prev?.snapshot) && prev.snapshot.length
00199:           ? prev.snapshot
00200:           : Array.isArray(snapshot)
00201:             ? snapshot
00202:             : [],
00203:       engineVersion:
00204:         safeStr(prev?.engineVersion || engineVersion || "V3_STATISTICAL"),
00205:       createdAt: prev.createdAt || Date.now(),
00206:       status,
00207:     };
00208:   } else {
00209:     log.push({
00210:       targetKey: normalizedKey,
00211:       target: { ymd, hour },
00212:       picks: normalizedPicks,
00213:       result: null,
00214:       hit: null,
00215:       snapshot: Array.isArray(snapshot) ? snapshot : [],
00216:       engineVersion: safeStr(engineVersion || "V3_STATISTICAL"),
00217:       createdAt: Date.now(),
00218:       status,
00219:     });
00220:   }
00221: 
00222:   saveTop3Log(log);
00223: }
00224: 
00225: function registerResult({ targetKey, resultGrupo }) {
00226:   const log = getTop3Log();
00227:   const normalizedTargetKey = safeStr(targetKey);
00228:   const result = normalizeResult(resultGrupo);
00229: 
00230:   if (!normalizedTargetKey || result == null) return;
00231: 
00232:   const idx = log.findIndex(
00233:     (l) => String(l?.targetKey || "") === String(normalizedTargetKey)
00234:   );
00235: 
00236:   if (idx === -1) return;
00237: 
00238:   const picks = normalizePicks(log[idx]?.picks);
00239: 
00240:   log[idx] = {
00241:     ...log[idx],
00242:     picks,
00243:     result,
00244:     hit: picks.includes(result),
00245:     validatedAt: Date.now(),
00246:     status: "validated",
00247:   };
00248: 
00249:   saveTop3Log(log);
00250: }
00251: 
00252: function findRealDrawByTarget({ targetYmd, targetHour, todayDraws, rangeDraws }) {
00253:   const ymd = safeStr(targetYmd);
00254:   const hour = normHour(targetHour);
00255: 
00256:   if (!isYMD(ymd) || !hour) return null;
00257: 
00258:   const sameTarget = (d) =>
00259:     pickDrawYMD(d) === ymd && normHour(pickDrawHour(d)) === hour;
00260: 
00261:   const realToday =
00262:     (Array.isArray(todayDraws) ? todayDraws : []).find(sameTarget) || null;
00263: 
00264:   const realRange =
00265:     (Array.isArray(rangeDraws) ? rangeDraws : []).find(sameTarget) || null;
00266: 
00267:   return realToday || realRange || null;
00268: }
00269: 
00270: function reconcilePendingTop3Log({ todayDraws, rangeDraws }) {
00271:   const log = getTop3Log();
00272:   if (!Array.isArray(log) || !log.length) return;
00273: 
00274:   let changed = false;
00275: 
00276:   const nextLog = log.map((entry) => {
00277:     if (!entry || entry.result != null) return entry;
00278: 
00279:     const targetYmd = safeStr(entry?.target?.ymd || "");
00280:     const targetHour = normHour(entry?.target?.hour || "");
00281: 
00282:     if (!isYMD(targetYmd) || !targetHour) return entry;
00283:     if (isFutureTarget(targetYmd, targetHour)) return entry;
00284: 
00285:     const real = findRealDrawByTarget({
00286:       targetYmd,
00287:       targetHour,
00288:       todayDraws,
00289:       rangeDraws,
00290:     });
00291: 
00292:     if (!real) return entry;
00293: 
00294:     const result = normalizeResult(pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(real));
00295:     if (result == null) return entry;
00296: 
00297:     const picks = normalizePicks(entry?.picks);
00298: 
00299:     changed = true;
00300: 
00301:     return {
00302:       ...entry,
00303:       picks,
00304:       result,
00305:       hit: picks.includes(result),
00306:       validatedAt: Date.now(),
00307:       status: "validated",
00308:     };
00309:   });
00310: 
00311:   if (changed) saveTop3Log(nextLog);
00312: }
00313: 
00314: function ensureDayTimeline({ ymd, lotteryKey }) {
00315:   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return;
00316: 
00317:   const schedule = getScheduleForLottery({
00318:     lotteryKey,
00319:     ymd,
00320:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00321:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
00322:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
00323:   });
00324: 
00325:   if (!Array.isArray(schedule) || !schedule.length) return;
00326: 
00327:   const log = getTop3Log();
00328: 
00329:   for (const h of schedule) {
00330:     const hour = normHour(h);
00331:     const targetKey = makeKey(ymd, hour);
00332: 
00333:     if (!targetKey) continue;
00334: 
00335:     const idx = log.findIndex(
00336:       (l) => String(l?.targetKey || "") === String(targetKey)
00337:     );
00338: 
00339:     if (idx >= 0) {
00340:       const prev = log[idx] || {};
00341:       const picks = normalizePicks(prev?.picks);
00342:       const result = normalizeResult(prev?.result);
00343:       const status =
00344:         result != null ? "validated" : resolvePendingStatus(ymd, hour, picks);
00345: 
00346:       log[idx] = {
00347:         ...prev,
00348:         targetKey,
00349:         target: { ymd, hour },
00350:         picks,
00351:         result,
00352:         hit: result != null ? picks.includes(result) : prev?.hit ?? null,
00353:         createdAt: prev?.createdAt || Date.now(),
00354:         validatedAt: result != null ? prev?.validatedAt : undefined,
00355:         status,
00356:       };
00357:     } else {
00358:       log.push({
00359:         targetKey,
00360:         target: { ymd, hour },
00361:         picks: [],
00362:         result: null,
00363:         hit: null,
00364:         createdAt: Date.now(),
00365:         status: "empty",
00366:       });
00367:     }
00368:   }
00369: 
00370:   saveTop3Log(log);
00371: }
00372: 
00373: export {
00374:   getTop3Log,
00375:   saveTop3Log,
00376:   registerPrediction,
00377:   registerResult,
00378:   reconcilePendingTop3Log,
00379:   ensureDayTimeline,
00380:   isFutureTarget,
00381:   makeKey,
00382: };

00038: function makePredictionId({ lotteryKey, targetYmd, targetHour }) {
00039:   const lottery = normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey);
00040:   const ymd = safeStr(targetYmd);
00041:   const hour = hourCode(targetHour);
00042: 
00043:   if (!lottery || !isYMD(ymd) || !hour) return "";
00044:

00053: function cleanFirestoreValue(value) {
00054:   try {
00055:     return JSON.parse(
00056:       JSON.stringify(value, (_key, current) => {
00057:         if (current === undefined) return null;
00058:         if (typeof current === "number" && !Number.isFinite(current)) {
00059:           return null;
00060:         }
00061:         return current;
00062:       })
00063:     );
00064:   } catch {
00065:     return null;
00066:   }
00067: }

00070:   const digits = String(value || "").replace(/\D/g, "");
00071:   if (!digits) return "";
00072:   return digits.slice(-4).padStart(4, "0");
00073: }

00080:     prizes[0] ||
00081:     null
00082:   );

00098: function findDrawForTarget({
00099:   draws,
00100:   targetYmd,

00104:   const hour = normalizeHour(targetHour);
00105:

00112:         normalizeHour(pickDrawHour(draw)) === hour

00117: function normalizeSnapshot(snapshot) {
00118:   return (Array.isArray(snapshot) ? snapshot : [])
00119:     .slice(0, 3)
00120:     .map((item, index) => ({
00121:       rank: Number(item?.rank || index + 1),
00122:       grupo: Number(item?.grupo),
00123:       animal: safeStr(item?.animal || ""),
00124:       prob: Number(item?.prob || 0),
00125:       probPct: Number(item?.probPct || 0),

00127:         ? item.milhares20.map(normalizeMilhar).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 24)

00130:         ? cleanFirestoreValue(item.milharesCols)
00131:         : [],
00132:       meta: cleanFirestoreValue(item?.meta || null),
00133:     }))
00134:     .filter((item) => {
00135:       return (
00136:         Number.isFinite(item.grupo) &&
00137:         item.grupo >= 1 &&
00138:         item.grupo <= 25
00139:       );
00140:     });

00143: function extractPrizeGrupo(prize) {
00144:   const direct = Number(
00145:     prize?.grupo ??
00146:       prize?.group ??
00147:       prize?.animal_grupo ??
00148:       prize?.grupo2
00149:   );
00150: 
00151:   if (
00152:     Number.isFinite(direct) &&
00153:     direct >= 1 &&
00154:     direct <= 25
00155:   ) {
00156:     return direct;
00157:   }
00158: 
00159:   const milhar = normalizeMilhar(
00160:     prize?.milhar ??
00161:       prize?.numero ??
00162:       prize?.number ??
00163:       prize?.valor ??
00164:       ""
00165:   );
00166: 
00167:   if (!milhar) return null;
00168: 
00169:   const dezena = Number(milhar.slice(-2));
00170:   const normalizedDezena = dezena === 0 ? 100 : dezena;
00171:   const grupo = Math.ceil(normalizedDezena / 4);
00172: 
00173:   return grupo >= 1 && grupo <= 25 ? grupo : null;
00174: }

00196:         prize?.valor ??

00202:       Number(grupo) < 1 ||
00203:       Number(grupo) > 25

00210:       grupo: Number(grupo),

00213:     };

00219:   if (Number(position) === 2) return "silver";
00220:   if (Number(position) === 3) return "bronze";

00224: function analyzeSnapshotHit(snapshot, officialPodium) {
00225:   const top3 = Array.isArray(snapshot)
00226:     ? snapshot.slice(0, 3)
00227:     : [];
00228: 
00229:   const podium = Array.isArray(officialPodium)
00230:     ? officialPodium.filter(Boolean).slice(0, 3)
00231:     : [];
00232: 
00233:   if (!top3.length || !podium.length) {
00234:     return {
00235:       hitType: "miss",
00236:       hitScore: 0,
00237:       hitPosition: -1,
00238:       predictionPosition: -1,
00239:       resultPosition: -1,
00240:       podiumMedal: "",
00241:       matchedValue: "",
00242:       matchedGrupo: null,
00243:       matchedMilhar: "",
00244:       matchedAnimal: "",
00245:     };
00246:   }
00247: 
00248:   for (const officialPrize of podium) {
00249:     const resultGrupo = Number(
00250:       officialPrize?.grupo
00251:     );
00252: 
00253:     const resultMilhar = normalizeMilhar(
00254:       officialPrize?.milhar
00255:     );
00256: 
00257:     const resultCentena = resultMilhar
00258:       ? resultMilhar.slice(-3)
00259:       : "";
00260: 
00261:     const resultDezena = resultMilhar
00262:       ? resultMilhar.slice(-2)
00263:       : "";
00264: 
00265:     for (
00266:       let predictionIndex = 0;
00267:       predictionIndex < top3.length;
00268:       predictionIndex += 1
00269:     ) {
00270:       const prediction = top3[predictionIndex];
00271:       const predictionGrupo = Number(
00272:         prediction?.grupo
00273:       );
00274: 
00275:       const milhares = (
00276:         Array.isArray(prediction?.milhares20)
00277:           ? prediction.milhares20
00278:           : Array.isArray(prediction?.milhares)
00279:             ? prediction.milhares
00280:             : []
00281:       )
00282:         .map(normalizeMilhar)
00283:         .filter(Boolean);
00284: 
00285:       const centenas = milhares.map(
00286:         (value) => value.slice(-3)
00287:       );
00288: 
00289:       const dezenas = milhares.map(
00290:         (value) => value.slice(-2)
00291:       );
00292: 
00293:       let hitType = "miss";
00294:       let hitScore = 0;
00295:       let matchedValue = "";
00296: 
00297:       if (
00298:         resultMilhar &&
00299:         milhares.includes(resultMilhar)
00300:       ) {
00301:         hitType = "hit_exact";
00302:         hitScore = 100;
00303:         matchedValue = resultMilhar;
00304:       } else if (
00305:         resultCentena &&
00306:         centenas.includes(resultCentena)
00307:       ) {
00308:         hitType = "hit_centena";
00309:         hitScore = 66.67;
00310:         matchedValue = resultCentena;
00311:       } else if (
00312:         resultDezena &&
00313:         dezenas.includes(resultDezena)
00314:       ) {
00315:         hitType = "hit_dezena";
00316:         hitScore = 33.33;
00317:         matchedValue = resultDezena;
00318:       } else if (
00319:         Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) &&
00320:         predictionGrupo === resultGrupo
00321:       ) {
00322:         hitType = "hit_grupo";
00323:         hitScore = 33.33;
00324:         matchedValue = String(
00325:           resultGrupo
00326:         ).padStart(2, "0");
00327:       }
00328: 
00329:       if (hitType !== "miss") {
00330:         const resultPosition = Number(
00331:           officialPrize?.position
00332:         );
00333: 
00334:         return {
00335:           hitType,
00336:           hitScore,
00337:           hitPosition: predictionIndex + 1,
00338:           predictionPosition: predictionIndex + 1,
00339:           resultPosition,
00340:           podiumMedal:
00341:             podiumMedalFromPosition(resultPosition),
00342:           matchedValue,
00343:           matchedGrupo: resultGrupo,
00344:           matchedMilhar: resultMilhar,
00345:           matchedAnimal: safeStr(
00346:             officialPrize?.animal || ""
00347:           ),
00348:         };
00349:       }
00350:     }
00351:   }
00352: 
00353:   return {
00354:     hitType: "miss",
00355:     hitScore: 0,
00356:     hitPosition: -1,
00357:     predictionPosition: -1,
00358:     resultPosition: -1,
00359:     podiumMedal: "",
00360:     matchedValue: "",
00361:     matchedGrupo: null,
00362:     matchedMilhar: "",
00363:     matchedAnimal: "",
00364:   };
00365: }

00367: export async function saveTop3PredictionSnapshot({
00368:   lotteryKey,
00369:   targetYmd,
00370:   targetHour,
00371:   picks,
00372:   snapshot,
00373:   engineVersion,
00374: }) {
00375:   let user = null;
00376: 
00377:   try {
00378:     user = await loginAnonymous();
00379:   } catch (error) {
00380:     return {
00381:       ok: false,
00382:       skipped: true,
00383:       reason: "AUTH_FAILED",
00384:       error: String(error?.message || error || ""),
00385:     };
00386:   }
00387: 
00388:   if (!user?.uid) {
00389:     return {
00390:       ok: false,
00391:       skipped: true,
00392:       reason: "AUTH_REQUIRED",
00393:     };
00394:   }
00395: 
00396:   const lottery = normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey);
00397:   const ymd = safeStr(targetYmd);
00398:   const hour = normalizeHour(targetHour);
00399:   const ref = predictionRef({
00400:     lotteryKey: lottery,
00401:     targetYmd: ymd,
00402:     targetHour: hour,
00403:   });
00404: 
00405:   if (!ref || !isYMD(ymd) || !hour) {
00406:     return {
00407:       ok: false,
00408:       skipped: true,
00409:       reason: "INVALID_TARGET",
00410:     };
00411:   }
00412: 
00413:   const normalizedSnapshot = normalizeSnapshot(snapshot);
00414: 
00415:   if (!normalizedSnapshot.length) {
00416:     return {
00417:       ok: false,
00418:       skipped: true,
00419:       reason: "EMPTY_SNAPSHOT",
00420:     };
00421:   }
00422: 
00423:   const normalizedPicks = Array.from(
00424:     new Set(
00425:       (Array.isArray(picks) ? picks : [])
00426:         .map(Number)
00427:         .filter((value) => value >= 1 && value <= 25)
00428:     )
00429:   ).slice(0, 3);
00430: 
00431:   const now = Date.now();
00432: 
00433:   const payload = cleanFirestoreValue({
00434:     id: ref.id,
00435:     lotteryKey: lottery,
00436:     targetYmd: ymd,
00437:     targetHour: hour,
00438:     targetKey: `${ymd}_${hour}`,
00439:     picks: normalizedPicks,
00440:     snapshot: normalizedSnapshot,
00441:     engineVersion: safeStr(engineVersion || "V3_STATISTICAL"),
00442:     status: "predicted",
00443:     resultGrupo: null,
00444:     resultMilhar: "",
00445:     resultAnimal: "",
00446:     hitType: "",
00447:     hitScore: 0,
00448:     hitPosition: -1,
00449:     matchedValue: "",
00450:     createdAt: now,
00451:     updatedAt: now,
00452:     createdBy: user.uid,
00453:   });
00454: 
00455:   const result = await runTransaction(db, async (transaction) => {
00456:     const current = await transaction.get(ref);
00457: 
00458:     if (current.exists()) {
00459:       return {
00460:         ok: true,
00461:         created: false,
00462:         existing: true,
00463:       };
00464:     }
00465: 
00466:     transaction.set(ref, payload);
00467: 
00468:     return {
00469:       ok: true,
00470:       created: true,
00471:       existing: false,
00472:     };
00473:   });
00474: 
00475:   return result;
00476: }
00477: 
00478: export async function loadTop3PredictionDay({
00479:   lotteryKey,
00480:   targetYmd,
00481:   schedule,
00482: }) {
00483:   const lottery = normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey);
00484:   const ymd = safeStr(targetYmd);
00485: 
00486:   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return [];
00487: 
00488:   const hours = Array.from(
00489:     new Set(
00490:       (Array.isArray(schedule) ? schedule : [])
00491:         .map(normalizeHour)
00492:         .filter(Boolean)
00493:     )
00494:   );
00495: 
00496:   const snapshots = await Promise.all(
00497:     hours.map(async (hour) => {
00498:       const ref = predictionRef({
00499:         lotteryKey: lottery,
00500:         targetYmd: ymd,
00501:         targetHour: hour,
00502:       });
00503: 
00504:       if (!ref) return null;
00505: 
00506:       try {
00507:         const snap = await getDoc(ref);
00508:         if (!snap.exists()) return null;
00509: 
00510:         return {
00511:           id: snap.id,
00512:           ...snap.data(),
00513:         };
00514:       } catch {
00515:         return null;
00516:       }
00517:     })
00518:   );
00519: 
00520:   return snapshots
00521:     .filter(Boolean)
00522:     .sort((a, b) => {
00523:       return normalizeHour(a?.targetHour).localeCompare(
00524:         normalizeHour(b?.targetHour)
00525:       );
00526:     });
00527: }

00529: export async function reconcileTop3PredictionDay({
00530:   lotteryKey,
00531:   targetYmd,
00532:   schedule,
00533:   draws,
00534: }) {
00535:   let user = null;
00536: 
00537:   try {
00538:     user = await loginAnonymous();
00539:   } catch (error) {
00540:     return {
00541:       ok: false,
00542:       skipped: true,
00543:       reason: "AUTH_FAILED",
00544:       error: String(error?.message || error || ""),
00545:     };
00546:   }
00547: 
00548:   if (!user?.uid) {
00549:     return {
00550:       ok: false,
00551:       skipped: true,
00552:       reason: "AUTH_REQUIRED",
00553:     };
00554:   }
00555: 
00556:   const history = await loadTop3PredictionDay({
00557:     lotteryKey,
00558:     targetYmd,
00559:     schedule,
00560:   });
00561: 
00562:   const lottery = normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey);
00563:   let updated = 0;
00564:   const reconciledHistory = [];
00565: 
00566:   for (const entry of history) {
00567:     if (!entry) continue;
00568: 
00569:     const realDraw = findDrawForTarget({
00570:       draws,
00571:       targetYmd: entry?.targetYmd,
00572:       targetHour: entry?.targetHour,
00573:     });
00574: 
00575:     if (!realDraw) {
00576:       reconciledHistory.push(entry);
00577:       continue;
00578:     }
00579: 
00580:     const officialPodium =
00581:       extractOfficialPodium(realDraw);
00582: 
00583:     const firstOfficialPrize =
00584:       officialPodium.find(
00585:         (item) => Number(item?.position) === 1
00586:       ) || null;
00587: 
00588:     const resultGrupo = Number(
00589:       firstOfficialPrize?.grupo
00590:     );
00591: 
00592:     if (
00593:       !Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) ||
00594:       resultGrupo < 1 ||
00595:       resultGrupo > 25
00596:     ) {
00597:       reconciledHistory.push(entry);
00598:       continue;
00599:     }
00600: 
00601:     const resultMilhar = normalizeMilhar(
00602:       firstOfficialPrize?.milhar
00603:     );
00604:     const savedLottery = safeStr(
00605:       entry?.resultLotteryKey
00606:     ).toUpperCase();
00607:     const savedGrupo = Number(entry?.resultGrupo);
00608:     const savedMilhar = normalizeMilhar(entry?.resultMilhar);
00609: 
00610:     const analysis = analyzeSnapshotHit(
00611:       entry?.snapshot,
00612:       officialPodium
00613:     );
00614: 
00615:     const alreadyMatchesRealResult =
00616:       entry?.status === "validated" &&
00617:       savedLottery === lottery &&
00618:       savedGrupo === resultGrupo &&
00619:       savedMilhar === resultMilhar &&
00620:       safeStr(entry?.hitType) === analysis.hitType &&
00621:       Number(entry?.hitScore) === analysis.hitScore &&
00622:       Number(entry?.hitPosition) === analysis.hitPosition &&
00623:       Number(entry?.resultPosition ?? -1) ===
00624:         Number(analysis.resultPosition ?? -1) &&
00625:       safeStr(entry?.podiumMedal) ===
00626:         safeStr(analysis.podiumMedal) &&
00627:       safeStr(entry?.matchedValue) === analysis.matchedValue;
00628: 
00629:     if (alreadyMatchesRealResult) {
00630:       reconciledHistory.push(entry);
00631:       continue;
00632:     }
00633: 
00634:     const ref = doc(db, COLLECTION, entry.id);
00635:     const now = Date.now();
00636: 
00637:     const validationPayload = {
00638:       resultGrupo,
00639:       resultMilhar,
00640:       resultLotteryKey: lottery,
00641:       resultAnimal: safeStr(
00642:         firstOfficialPrize?.animal ||
00643:           extractPrize1(realDraw)?.animal ||
00644:           ""
00645:       ),
00646:       resultTop3Groups: officialPodium.map(
00647:         (item) => Number(item?.grupo) || null
00648:       ),
00649:       resultTop3Milhares: officialPodium.map(
00650:         (item) => normalizeMilhar(item?.milhar)
00651:       ),
00652:       hitType: analysis.hitType,
00653:       hitScore: analysis.hitScore,
00654:       hitPosition: analysis.hitPosition,
00655:       predictionPosition: analysis.predictionPosition,
00656:       resultPosition: analysis.resultPosition,
00657:       podiumMedal: analysis.podiumMedal,
00658:       matchedGrupo: analysis.matchedGrupo,
00659:       matchedMilhar: analysis.matchedMilhar,
00660:       matchedAnimal: analysis.matchedAnimal,
00661:       matchedValue: analysis.matchedValue,
00662:       validatedAt: now,
00663:       validatedBy: user.uid,
00664:       updatedAt: now,
00665:       status: "validated",
00666:     };
00667: 
00668:     await setDoc(
00669:       ref,
00670:       validationPayload,
00671:       { merge: true }
00672:     );
00673: 
00674:     reconciledHistory.push({
00675:       ...entry,
00676:       ...validationPayload,
00677:     });
00678: 
00679:     updated += 1;
00680:   }
00681: 
00682:   return {
00683:     ok: true,
00684:     updated,
00685:     history: reconciledHistory,
00686:   };
00687: }

00018: function clampProb(v) {
00019:   const n = Number(v);
00020:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;
00021:   if (n > 1) return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n / 100));
00022:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n));
00023: }
00024: 
00025: function resolveProbValue(x) {
00026:   return clampProb(x?.scoreProb ?? x?.prob ?? x?.probCond ?? 0);
00027: }
00028: 
00029: function normalizeMilhar4(v) {
00030:   const dig = String(v || "").replace(/\D+/g, "");
00031:   if (!dig) return "";
00032:   return dig.length >= 4 ? dig.slice(-4) : dig.padStart(4, "0");
00033: }
00034: 
00035: function build4ColsFromEngineOut(out, expectedCols = 4, perCol = 5) {
00036:   const dezenas = Array.isArray(out?.dezenas) ? out.dezenas : [];
00037:   const slots = Array.isArray(out?.slots) ? out.slots : [];
00038:   const cols = [];
00039: 
00040:   for (const dz of dezenas.slice(0, expectedCols)) {
00041:     const items = slots
00042:       .filter((s) => String(s?.dezena || "") === String(dz))
00043:       .map((s) => normalizeMilhar4(s?.milhar))
00044:       .map((m) => (/^\d{4}$/.test(m) ? m : ""))
00045:       .slice(0, perCol);
00046: 
00047:     while (items.length < perCol) items.push("");
00048:     cols.push({ dezena: dz, items });
00049:   }
00050: 
00051:   while (cols.length < expectedCols) {
00052:     cols.push({ dezena: "", items: Array(perCol).fill("") });
00053:   }
00054: 
00055:   return cols.slice(0, expectedCols);
00056: }

00058: function normalizeImgSrc(src, publicBase = "") {
00059:   const s = String(src || "").trim();
00060:   if (!s) return "";
00061:   if (/^(https?:)?\/\//i.test(s)) return s;
00062:   if (/^(data:|blob:)/i.test(s)) return s;
00063: 
00064:   const base = String(publicBase || "").trim().replace(/\/+$/, "");
00065: 
00066:   if (s.startsWith("/")) return `${base}${s}`;
00067:   if (s.startsWith("public/")) return `${base}/${s.slice("public/".length)}`;
00068:   return `${base}/${s}`;
00069: }
00070: 
00071: function buildResultStyleImgVariants(grupo, publicBase = "", size = 96) {
00072:   const g = Number(grupo);
00073:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) return [];
00074: 
00075:   const seeds = [getImgFromGrupo?.(g, size), getImgFromGrupo?.(g)]
00076:     .map((x) => normalizeImgSrc(x, publicBase))
00077:     .filter(Boolean);
00078: 
00079:   const out = [];
00080: 
00081:   for (const seed of seeds) {
00082:     const clean = String(seed).split("?")[0];
00083:     if (!clean) continue;
00084: 
00085:     out.push(clean);
00086: 
00087:     if (/\.png$/i.test(clean)) {
00088:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.png$/i, ".jpg"));
00089:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.png$/i, ".jpeg"));
00090:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.png$/i, ".webp"));
00091:     } else if (/\.jpg$/i.test(clean)) {
00092:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.jpg$/i, ".png"));
00093:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.jpg$/i, ".jpeg"));
00094:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.jpg$/i, ".webp"));
00095:     } else if (/\.jpeg$/i.test(clean)) {
00096:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.jpeg$/i, ".png"));
00097:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.jpeg$/i, ".jpg"));
00098:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.jpeg$/i, ".webp"));
00099:     } else if (/\.webp$/i.test(clean)) {
00100:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.webp$/i, ".png"));
00101:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.webp$/i, ".jpg"));
00102:       out.push(clean.replace(/\.webp$/i, ".jpeg"));
00103:     }
00104:   }
00105: 
00106:   return Array.from(new Set(out.filter(Boolean)));
00107: }
00108: 
00109: function top3ItemMatchesSlot(item, slot) {
00110:   const targetY = safeStr(slot?.targetYmd || "");
00111:   const targetH = toHourBucket(slot?.targetHour || "");
00112: 
00113:   const nextY = safeStr(item?.meta?.next?.ymd || "");
00114:   const nextH = toHourBucket(item?.meta?.next?.hour || "");
00115: 
00116:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH) return false;
00117:   if (!isYMD(nextY) || !nextH) return false;
00118: 
00119:   return nextY === targetY && nextH === targetH;
00120: }

00122: export function buildTop3TimelineViewModel({
00123:   todayDraws,
00124:   rangeDraws,
00125:   lotteryKeySafe,
00126:   ymdSafe,
00127:   analysisYmd,
00128:   publicBase = "",
00129: }) {
00130:   const day = Array.isArray(todayDraws) ? todayDraws : [];
00131:   const range = Array.isArray(rangeDraws) ? rangeDraws : [];
00132: 
00133:   const safeAnalysisYmd = safeStr(analysisYmd || "");
00134:   const safeYmd = safeStr(ymdSafe || "");
00135:   // O calendario deve permanecer ancorado na data escolhida.
00136:   // Isso preserva o dia anterior quando o proximo alvo cruza a meia-noite.
00137:   const timelineYmd = isYMD(safeYmd) ? safeYmd : safeAnalysisYmd;
00138: 
00139:   if (!isYMD(timelineYmd) || !range.length) return [];
00140: 
00141:   const rawTimeline = buildTimelineTop3({
00142:     ymd: timelineYmd,
00143:     drawsToday: day,
00144:     drawsRange: range,
00145:     lotteryKey: lotteryKeySafe,
00146:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00147:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
00148:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
00149:   });
00150: 
00151:   const timelineAudit = auditTop3Timeline({
00152:     timeline: rawTimeline,
00153:     lotteryKey: lotteryKeySafe,
00154:   });
00155: 
00156:   if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
00157:     window.__TOP3_AUDIT__ = timelineAudit;
00158: 
00159:     console.groupCollapsed("[TOP3 AUDIT]");
00160:     console.table([
00161:       {
00162:         Loteria: timelineAudit.lotteryKey,
00163:         Previsoes: timelineAudit.total,
00164:         "TOP1 %": timelineAudit.top1Rate,
00165:         "TOP3 %": timelineAudit.top3Rate,
00166:       },
00167:     ]);
00168: 
00169:     console.log("Por horário");
00170:     console.table(timelineAudit.byHour);
00171: 
00172:     console.log("Por data");
00173:     console.table(timelineAudit.byDate);
00174: 
00175:     console.groupEnd();
00176:   }
00177: 
00178: 
00179:   const milharesCache = new Map();
00180: 
00181:   return (Array.isArray(rawTimeline) ? rawTimeline : []).map((slot) => {
00182:     const arr = Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3 : [];
00183: 
00184:     const slotYmd = safeStr(slot?.targetYmd || "");
00185:     const slotHour = toHourBucket(slot?.targetHour || "");
00186: 
00187:     const mappedTop3 = arr
00188:       .filter((x) => {
00189:         const g = Number(x?.grupo);
00190:         return Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= 25;
00191:       })
00192:       .filter((x) => top3ItemMatchesSlot(x, slot))
00193:       .slice(0, 3)
00194:       .map((x) => {
00195:         const g = Number(x?.grupo);
00196:         const animal = safeStr(getAnimalLabel(g) || "");
00197: 
00198:         const cacheKey = [g, slotYmd, slotHour].join("|");
00199: 
00200:         let out = milharesCache.get(cacheKey);
00201: 
00202:         if (!out) {
00203:           out = buildMilharesForGrupo({
00204:             rangeDraws: range,
00205:             analysisHourBucket: slotHour,
00206:             schedule: getScheduleForLottery({
00207:               lotteryKey: lotteryKeySafe,
00208:               ymd: slotYmd,
00209:               PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00210:               PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
00211:               FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
00212:             }),
00213:             grupo2: g,
00214:             count: 20,
00215:             targetYmd: slotYmd,
00216:           });
00217: 
00218:           milharesCache.set(cacheKey, out);
00219:         }
00220: 
00221:         const milharesCols = build4ColsFromEngineOut(out, 4, 5);
00222:         const milhares20 = milharesCols.flatMap((c) => c.items).slice(0, 20);
00223: 
00224:         const prob = resolveProbValue(x);
00225: 
00226:         const bgPrimary = normalizeImgSrc(
00227:           safeStr(getImgFromGrupo?.(g, 512) || getImgFromGrupo?.(g) || ""),
00228:           publicBase
00229:         );
00230: 
00231:         return {
00232:           ...x,
00233:           animal,
00234:           imgBg: bgPrimary ? [bgPrimary] : [],
00235:           imgIcon: buildResultStyleImgVariants(g, publicBase, 96),
00236:           prob,
00237:           probPct: prob * 100,
00238:           meta: x?.meta || null,
00239:           milharesCols,
00240:           milhares20,
00241:         };
00242:       });
00243: 
00244:     return {
00245:       ...slot,
00246:       top3: mappedTop3,
00247:     };
00248:   });
00249: }

00001: export async function fallbackBaseSearch({
00002:   getKingResultsByRange,
00003:   findLatestHistoricalBaseDraw,
00004:   addDaysYMD,
00005:   minDate,
00006:   lotteryKey,
00007:   targetYmd,

00012:   const safeUf = String(uf || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00013:   const safeTargetYmd = String(targetYmd || "").trim();
00014:   const safeTargetHour = String(targetHourBucket || "").trim();
00015:

00017:     return { draw: null, ymd: "", hour: "", source: "none" };
00018:   }

00021:     String(minDate || "").trim() ||
00022:     (key === "FEDERAL"
00023:       ? addDaysYMD(safeTargetYmd, -240)

00027:     (await getKingResultsByRange({
00028:       uf: safeUf,

00032:       readPolicy: "cache",
00033:     })) || [];
00034: 
00035:   return findLatestHistoricalBaseDraw({
00036:     draws: hist,

00001: import { pad2, safeStr } from "./top3.formatters";
00002: 
00003: function publicBase() {
00004:   const b = String(process.env.PUBLIC_URL || "").trim();
00005:   return b && b !== "/" ? b.replace(/\/+$/, "") : "";
00006: }
00007: 
00008: function joinPublic(base, path) {
00009:   if (!path) return "";
00010: 
00011:   const p = path.startsWith("/") ? path : `/${path}`;
00012: 
00013:   if (!base) return p;
00014: 
00015:   return `${base}${p}`;
00016: }
00017: 
00018: export function normalizeImgSrc(src) {
00019:   const s0 = safeStr(src);
00020:   if (!s0) return "";
00021: 
00022:   if (/^(https?:)?\/\//i.test(s0)) return s0;
00023:   if (/^(data:|blob:)/i.test(s0)) return s0;
00024: 
00025:   const base = publicBase();
00026: 
00027:   let s = s0;
00028: 
00029:   s = s.replace(/^(\.\/)+/, "");
00030:   s = s.replace(/^(\.\.\/)+/, "");
00031: 
00032:   if (base) {
00033:     if (s === base) return s;
00034:     if (s.startsWith(base + "/")) return s;
00035:   }
00036: 
00037:   if (s.startsWith("/")) return joinPublic(base, s);
00038:   if (s.startsWith("public/")) return joinPublic(base, s.slice("public/".length));
00039: 
00040:   return joinPublic(base, s);
00041: }
00042: 
00043: function slugifyLabel(label) {
00044:   const l = safeStr(label);
00045:   if (!l) return "";
00046: 
00047:   return l
00048:     .normalize("NFD")
00049:     .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
00050:     .toLowerCase()
00051:     .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "_")
00052:     .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "");
00053: }
00054: 
00055: function stripQuery(url) {
00056:   const s = safeStr(url);
00057:   if (!s) return "";
00058: 
00059:   const q = s.indexOf("?");
00060:   const h = s.indexOf("#");
00061: 
00062:   const cut =
00063:     q >= 0 && h >= 0
00064:       ? Math.min(q, h)
00065:       : q >= 0
00066:         ? q
00067:         : h >= 0
00068:           ? h
00069:           : -1;
00070: 
00071:   return cut >= 0 ? s.slice(0, cut) : s;
00072: }
00073: 
00074: function addCacheBust(url, token = "v=1") {
00075:   const s = safeStr(url);
00076:   if (!s) return "";
00077: 
00078:   return s.includes("?") ? `${s}&${token}` : `${s}?${token}`;
00079: }
00080: 
00081: function pushExtensionVariants(out, clean) {
00082:   const s = safeStr(clean);
00083:   if (!s) return;
00084: 
00085:   out.push(s);
00086: 
00087:   const m = s.match(/\.(png|jpg|jpeg|webp)$/i);
00088:   if (!m) return;
00089: 
00090:   const base = s.slice(0, -m[0].length);
00091: 
00092:   out.push(`${base}.png`);
00093:   out.push(`${base}.jpg`);
00094:   out.push(`${base}.jpeg`);
00095:   out.push(`${base}.webp`);
00096: }
00097: 
00098: export function makeImgVariantsFromGrupo({
00099:   grupo,
00100:   size,
00101:   getImgFromGrupo,
00102:   getAnimalLabel,
00103: }) {
00104:   const g = Number(grupo);
00105: 
00106:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) return [];
00107: 
00108:   const s = Math.max(1, Number(size) || 96);
00109: 
00110:   const base = publicBase();
00111:   const g2 = pad2(g);
00112: 
00113:   const label = safeStr(getAnimalLabel?.(g) || "");
00114:   const slug = slugifyLabel(label);
00115: 
00116:   const primary = normalizeImgSrc(
00117:     getImgFromGrupo?.(g, s) || getImgFromGrupo?.(g) || ""
00118:   );
00119: 
00120:   const seeds = [
00121:     primary,
00122: 
00123:     slug ? joinPublic(base, `img/${slug}_${s}.png`) : "",
00124:     slug ? joinPublic(base, `img/${slug}_${s}.jpg`) : "",
00125:     slug ? joinPublic(base, `img/${slug}_${s}.jpeg`) : "",
00126:     slug ? joinPublic(base, `img/${slug}_${s}.webp`) : "",
00127: 
00128:     joinPublic(base, `img/g${g2}_${s}.png`),
00129:     joinPublic(base, `img/g${g2}_${s}.jpg`),
00130:     joinPublic(base, `img/g${g2}_${s}.jpeg`),
00131:     joinPublic(base, `img/g${g2}_${s}.webp`),
00132:   ].filter(Boolean);
00133: 
00134:   const out = [];
00135: 
00136:   for (const seed of seeds) {
00137:     const original = safeStr(seed);
00138:     if (!original) continue;
00139: 
00140:     out.push(original);
00141: 
00142:     const clean = stripQuery(original);
00143:     if (!clean) continue;
00144: 
00145:     pushExtensionVariants(out, clean);
00146: 
00147:     out.push(addCacheBust(clean, "v=1"));
00148:   }
00149: 
00150:   return Array.from(new Set(out.filter(Boolean)));
00151: }
00152: 
00153: export function lotteryLabel(lotteryKey) {
00154:   const k = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
00155: 
00156:   if (k === "FEDERAL") return "FEDERAL (20h • qua/sáb)";
00157:   if (k === "PT_RIO") return "RIO (PT_RIO)";
00158: 
00159:   return k || "—";
00160: }

00197: /**
00198:  * ✅ Normaliza "horário" para HH:MM
00199:  */
00200: function normalizeHourLike(value) {
00201:   const s0 = String(value ?? "").trim();
00202:   if (!s0) return "";
00203: 
00204:   const s = s0.replace(/\s+/g, "");
00205: 
00206:   const mhx = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)$/i);
00207:   if (mhx) return `${pad2(mhx[1])}:00`;
00208: 
00209:   const mISO = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{1,2}))?$/);
00210:   if (mISO) return `${pad2(mISO[1])}:${pad2(mISO[2])}`;
00211: 
00212:   const mHm = s.match(/^(\d{3,4})$/);
00213:   if (mHm) {
00214:     const hh = String(mHm[1]).slice(0, -2);
00215:     const mm = String(mHm[1]).slice(-2);
00216:     if (/^\d{1,2}$/.test(hh) && /^\d{2}$/.test(mm)) {
00217:       return `${pad2(hh)}:${mm}`;
00218:     }
00219:   }
00220: 
00221:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
00222:   if (m2) return `${pad2(m2[1])}:00`;
00223: 
00224:   return s0.trim();
00225: }

00227: /**
00228:  * Converte o horário técnico/original da Nacional para o horário
00229:  * comercial exibido e utilizado pelos filtros do sistema.
00230:  *
00231:  * Histórico da fonte:
00232:  * 01:49 -> 02:00
00233:  * 07:49 -> 08:00
00234:  * 09:49 -> 10:00
00235:  * 11:49 -> 12:00
00236:  * 14:49 -> 15:00
00237:  * 16:49 -> 17:00
00238:  * 20:49 -> 21:00
00239:  * 22:49 -> 23:00
00240:  */
00241: function normalizeCloseHourForLottery(lotteryInput, value) {
00242:   const normalized = normalizeHourLike(value);
00243:   if (!normalized) return "";
00244: 
00245:   const lotteryKey = canonicalScopeKey(lotteryInput);
00246: 
00247:   if (lotteryKey !== "NACIONAL") {
00248:     return normalized;
00249:   }
00250: 
00251:   const nacionalHourMap = {
00252:     "01:49": "02:00",
00253:     "07:49": "08:00",
00254:     "09:49": "10:00",
00255:     "11:49": "12:00",
00256:     "14:49": "15:00",
00257:     "16:49": "17:00",
00258:     "20:49": "21:00",
00259:     "22:49": "23:00",
00260:   };
00261: 
00262:   return nacionalHourMap[normalized] || normalized;
00263: }

00265: function mhxToInt(v) {
00266:   const n = Number(v);
00267:   return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
00268: }

00270: /**
00271:  * ✅ Bucket "09h", "11h"…
00272:  */
00273: function toHourBucket(value) {
00274:   const s = String(value ?? "").trim();
00275:   if (!s || s.toLowerCase() === "todos") return null;
00276: 
00277:   const mh = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})h$/i);
00278:   if (mh) return `${pad2(mhxToInt(mh[1]))}h`;
00279: 
00280:   const m1 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/);
00281:   if (m1) return `${pad2(mhxToInt(m1[1]))}h`;
00282: 
00283:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
00284:   if (m2) return `${pad2(mhxToInt(m2[1]))}h`;
00285: 
00286:   const m3 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
00287:   if (m3) return `${pad2(mhxToInt(m3[1]))}h`;
00288: 
00289:   const m4 = s.match(/^(\d{3,4})$/);
00290:   if (m4) return `${pad2(mhxToInt(String(m4[1]).slice(0, -2)))}h`;
00291: 
00292:   return null;
00293: }
00294: 
00295: /* =========================
00296:    ✅ FIX DEFINITIVO: filtro de horário (bucket vs exato)
00297:    - bucket SOMENTE via closeHourBucket
00298:    - exact  SOMENTE via closeHour
00299:    (elimina ambiguidade e "filtro fantasma")
00300: ========================= */
00301: 
00302: function resolveHourFilter({ closeHour = null, closeHourBucket = null }) {
00303:   const bucket = toHourBucket(closeHourBucket);
00304:   if (bucket) return { kind: "bucket", bucket, hhmm: null };
00305: 
00306:   const hhmm = closeHour ? normalizeHourLike(closeHour) : null;
00307:   if (hhmm) return { kind: "exact", bucket: null, hhmm };
00308: 
00309:   return { kind: null, bucket: null, hhmm: null };
00310: }
00311: 
00312: function drawPassesHourFilter(draw, hourFilter) {
00313:   if (!hourFilter || !hourFilter.kind) return true;
00314: 
00315:   const raw =
00316:     draw?.close_hour ?? draw?.closeHour ?? draw?.hour ?? draw?.hora ?? "";
00317:   const norm = normalizeHourLike(raw);
00318: 
00319:   if (hourFilter.kind === "exact") {
00320:     return norm === normalizeHourLike(hourFilter.hhmm);
00321:   }
00322: 
00323:   if (hourFilter.kind === "bucket") {
00324:     return toHourBucket(norm) === toHourBucket(hourFilter.bucket);
00325:   }
00326: 
00327:   return true;
00328: }

00351:     d?.lotCode ??

00359: function sortDrawsLocal(draws) {
00360:   const toNum = (h) => {
00361:     const s = normalizeHourLike(h);
00362:     const m = s.match(/^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
00363:     if (!m) return 0;
00364:     return Number(m[1]) * 100 + Number(m[2]);
00365:   };
00366: 
00367:   return [...(draws || [])].sort((a, b) => {
00368:     const ya = String(a?.ymd || "");
00369:     const yb = String(b?.ymd || "");
00370:     if (ya !== yb) return ya.localeCompare(yb);
00371: 
00372:     const ha = toNum(a?.close_hour);
00373:     const hb = toNum(b?.close_hour);
00374:     if (ha !== hb) return ha - hb;
00375: 
00376:     const la = normalizeLotteryCodeAny(a);
00377:     const lb = normalizeLotteryCodeAny(b);
00378:     if (la !== lb) return la.localeCompare(lb);
00379: 
00380:     const ia = String(a?.drawId || a?.id || "");
00381:     const ib = String(b?.drawId || b?.id || "");
00382:     return ia.localeCompare(ib);
00383:   });
00384: }
00385: 
00386: function sortDrawsLocalDesc(draws) {
00387:   const toNum = (h) => {
00388:     const s = normalizeHourLike(h);
00389:     const m = s.match(/^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
00390:     if (!m) return 0;
00391:     return Number(m[1]) * 100 + Number(m[2]);
00392:   };
00393: 
00394:   return [...(draws || [])].sort((a, b) => {
00395:     const ya = String(a?.ymd || "");
00396:     const yb = String(b?.ymd || "");
00397:     if (ya !== yb) return yb.localeCompare(ya);
00398: 
00399:     const ha = toNum(a?.close_hour);
00400:     const hb = toNum(b?.close_hour);
00401:     if (ha !== hb) return hb - ha;
00402: 
00403:     const la = normalizeLotteryCodeAny(a);
00404:     const lb = normalizeLotteryCodeAny(b);
00405:     if (la !== lb) return lb.localeCompare(la);
00406: 
00407:     const ia = String(a?.drawId || a?.id || "");
00408:     const ib = String(b?.drawId || b?.id || "");
00409:     return ib.localeCompare(ia);
00410:   });
00411: }
00412: 
00413: /**
00414:  * ✅ DEDUPE
00415:  * - chave lógica: ymd + hour (+ lottery_code quando existir)
00416:  * - se não houver ymd/hour, cai no id/idx
00417:  *
00418:  * ✅ FIX CRÍTICO:
00419:  * Não colapsar dois draws distintos do mesmo dia/horário com lottery_code diferente.
00420:  */
00421: function dedupeDrawsLocal(draws) {
00422:   const arr = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
00423: 
00424:   const byKey = new Map();
00425:   const order = [];
00426: 
00427:   function score(d) {
00428:     const prizesLen = Array.isArray(d?.prizes) ? d.prizes.length : 0;
00429:     const pc = Number.isFinite(Number(d?.prizesCount))
00430:       ? Number(d.prizesCount)
00431:       : 0;
00432:     const hasLogical = !!(d?.ymd && d?.close_hour);
00433:     return prizesLen * 1_000_000 + pc * 1_000 + (hasLogical ? 10 : 0);
00434:   }
00435: 
00436:   for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i += 1) {
00437:     const raw = arr[i] || {};
00438: 
00439:     const ymd = raw.ymd || normalizeToYMD(raw.date) || "";
00440:     const hour = normalizeHourLike(raw.close_hour || raw.closeHour || "");
00441: 
00442:     const lotCode = normalizeLotteryCodeAny(raw);
00443: 
00444:     const drawId = raw.drawId ?? raw.id ?? raw.__name__ ?? null;
00445:     const idStr = drawId != null ? String(drawId) : "";
00446: 
00447:     const hasLogical = !!(ymd && hour);
00448: 
00449:     const key = hasLogical
00450:       ? lotCode
00451:         ? `${ymd}__${hour}__${lotCode}`
00452:         : `${ymd}__${hour}`
00453:       : `id__${idStr || `idx_${i}`}`;
00454: 
00455:     const normalized = {
00456:       ...raw,
00457:       ymd: ymd || raw.ymd || null,
00458:       close_hour: hour || raw.close_hour || raw.closeHour || "",
00459:       closeHour: hour || raw.closeHour || raw.close_hour || "",
00460:       lottery_code: lotCode || raw.lottery_code || raw.lotteryCode || null,
00461:       drawId: raw.drawId || raw.id || raw.__name__ || null,
00462:       id: raw.id || raw.drawId || raw.__name__ || null,
00463:     };
00464: 
00465:     if (!byKey.has(key)) {
00466:       byKey.set(key, normalized);
00467:       order.push(key);
00468:       continue;
00469:     }
00470: 
00471:     const prev = byKey.get(key);
00472:     if (score(normalized) > score(prev)) {
00473:       byKey.set(key, normalized);
00474:     }
00475:   }
00476: 
00477:   return order.map((k) => byKey.get(k)).filter(Boolean);
00478: }
00479: 
00480: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
00481:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
00482:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 6);
00483:   const results = new Array(arr.length);
00484:   let idx = 0;
00485: 
00486:   async function worker() {
00487:     while (true) {
00488:       const current = idx;
00489:       idx += 1;
00490:       if (current >= arr.length) break;
00491:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
00492:     }
00493:   }
00494: 
00495:   await Promise.all(
00496:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, () => worker())
00497:   );
00498:   return results;
00499: }
00500: 
00501: /* =========================
00502:    ✅ Leitura: smart (custo baixo)
00503: ========================= */
00504: 
00505: function isIndexError(error) {
00506:   const msg = String(error?.message || "").toLowerCase();
00507:   const code = String(error?.code || "").toLowerCase();
00508:   return (
00509:     code.includes("failed-precondition") ||
00510:     msg.includes("failed_precondition") ||
00511:     (msg.includes("index") && msg.includes("create")) ||
00512:     (msg.includes("requires") && msg.includes("index"))
00513:   );
00514: }
00515: 
00516: function getDocDateRaw(d) {
00517:   if (!d) return null;
00518:   return d.ymd ?? d.date ?? d.data ?? d.dt ?? d.draw_date ?? d.close_date ?? null;
00519: }
00520: 
00521: /* =========================
00522:    Prizes (ROBUSTO + CACHE)
00523: ========================= */
00524: 
00525: function prizesCacheKeyAll(drawId) {
00526:   return `all::${String(drawId)}`;
00527: }
00528: 
00529: /**
00530:  * ✅ FIX DEFINITIVO:
00531:  * Se existir embeddedPrizes mas vier "agregado/incompleto" (sem grupo/posição válidos),
00532:  * faz fallback e busca a subcollection draws/{id}/prizes.
00533:  */
00534: async function fetchPrizesForDraw(drawId, positionsArr, embeddedPrizes) {
00535:   const drawKey = String(drawId || "").trim();
00536:   if (!drawKey) return [];
00537: 
00538:   if (Array.isArray(embeddedPrizes) && embeddedPrizes.length) {
00539:     const normalized = embeddedPrizes.map((p, idx) =>
00540:       normalizePrize(p, p?.prizeId ?? `emb_${idx}`)
00541:     );
00542: 
00543:     const cleaned = normalized.filter(
00544:       (x) => isValidGrupo(x?.grupo) && isValidPosition(x?.position)
00545:     );
00546: 
00547:     if (cleaned.length) {
00548:       const allSorted = sortPrizesByPosition(cleaned);
00549:       return filterPrizesByPositions(allSorted, positionsArr);
00550:     }
00551:   }
00552: 
00553:   const allKey = prizesCacheKeyAll(drawKey);
00554:   const cachedAll = cacheGet(PRIZES_CACHE, allKey);
00555:   if (Array.isArray(cachedAll) && cachedAll.length > 0) {
00556:     return filterPrizesByPositions(cachedAll, positionsArr);
00557:   }
00558: 
00559:   const prizesCol = collection(db, "draws", drawKey, "prizes");
00560: 
00561:   const { snap, error } = await safeGetDocsSmart(prizesCol, { policy: "server" });
00562:   if (error) throw error;
00563: 
00564:   const allRaw = snap.docs.map((d) => normalizePrize(d.data(), d.id));
00565: 
00566:   // console.log("DEBUG allRaw:", allRaw);
00567: 
00568: const all = allRaw.filter(
00569:   (x) => isValidGrupo(x?.grupo) && isValidPosition(x?.position)
00570: );
00571: 
00572: // console.log("DEBUG allFiltered:", all);
00573: 
00574:   const allSorted = sortPrizesByPosition(all);
00575:   cacheSet(PRIZES_CACHE, allKey, allSorted);
00576: 
00577:   return filterPrizesByPositions(allSorted, positionsArr);
00578: }
00579: 
00580: /* =========================
00581:    Map Draw
00582: ========================= */
00583: 
00584: function mapDrawDoc(doc) {
00585:   const d = doc.data();
00586: 
00587:   const ymd = d.ymd || normalizeToYMD(getDocDateRaw(d));
00588: 
00589:   const ufRaw = d.uf ?? null;
00590:   const lotteryKeyRaw = d.lottery_key ?? d.lotteryKey ?? d.lottery ?? null;
00591: 
00592:   const hourNorm = normalizeCloseHourForLottery(
00593:     lotteryKeyRaw || ufRaw,
00594:     d.close_hour ?? d.closeHour ?? d.hour ?? d.hora ?? ""
00595:   );
00596: 
00597:   const embeddedPrizes = Array.isArray(d.prizes) && d.prizes.length > 0 ? d.prizes : null;
00598: 
00599:   const lotteryCodeRaw =
00600:     d.lottery_code ??
00601:     d.lotteryCode ??
00602:     d.lot_code ??
00603:     d.lotCode ??
00604:     d.lot ??
00605:     d.code ??
00606:     d.lottery_id ??
00607:     d.lotteryId ??
00608:     null;
00609: 
00610:   return {
00611:     drawId: doc.id,
00612:     id: doc.id,
00613: 
00614:     date: d.date ?? d.data ?? d.dt ?? d.draw_date ?? d.close_date ?? null,
00615:     ymd,
00616: 
00617:     close_hour: hourNorm,
00618:     closeHour: hourNorm,
00619: 
00620:     uf: ufRaw,
00621:     lottery_key: lotteryKeyRaw,
00622: 
00623:     lottery_code: lotteryCodeRaw ? String(lotteryCodeRaw).toUpperCase() : null,
00624: 
00625:     lottery_name: d.lottery_name ?? d.lotteryName ?? null,
00626:     lottery_label: d.lottery_label ?? d.lotteryLabel ?? null,
00627: 
00628:     prizesCount: d.prizesCount,
00629:     prizes: embeddedPrizes,
00630:   };
00631: }
