00633: /* =========================
00634:    Compat: uf OU lottery_key
00635: ========================= */
00636: 
00637: function buildUfWhereClauses(fieldName, uf) {
00638:   const normalized = normalizeValueForField(fieldName, uf);
00639:   return [where(fieldName, "==", normalized)];
00640: }
00641: 
00642: function normalizeOrderBys(extraOrderBy) {
00643:   if (!extraOrderBy) return [];
00644:   return Array.isArray(extraOrderBy)
00645:     ? extraOrderBy.filter(Boolean)
00646:     : [extraOrderBy];
00647: }
00648: 
00649: async function queryDrawsByField({
00650:   fieldName,
00651:   uf,
00652:   extraWheres = [],
00653:   extraOrderBy = null,
00654:   extraLimit = null,
00655:   policy = DEFAULT_READ_POLICY,
00656: }) {
00657:   const drawsCol = collection(db, "draws");
00658: 
00659:   const parts = [
00660:     drawsCol,
00661:     ...buildUfWhereClauses(fieldName, uf),
00662:     ...buildRJLockWhereIfNeeded(fieldName, uf),
00663:     ...(extraWheres || []),
00664:   ];
00665: 
00666:   const orderBys = normalizeOrderBys(extraOrderBy);
00667:   for (const ob of orderBys) parts.push(ob);
00668: 
00669:   if (extraLimit) parts.push(extraLimit);
00670: 
00671:   const qRef = query(...parts);
00672:   return safeGetDocsSmart(qRef, { policy });
00673: }
00674: 
00675: function extractUfParam(maybeUfOrObj) {
00676:   if (!maybeUfOrObj) return "";
00677:   if (typeof maybeUfOrObj === "string") return maybeUfOrObj;
00678:   if (typeof maybeUfOrObj === "object" && typeof maybeUfOrObj.uf === "string") {
00679:     return maybeUfOrObj.uf;
00680:   }
00681:   return String(maybeUfOrObj || "");
00682: }
00683: 
00684: async function fetchDrawDocsPreferUf({
00685:   uf,
00686:   extraWheres = [],
00687:   extraOrderBy = null,
00688:   extraLimit = null,
00689:   policy = DEFAULT_READ_POLICY,
00690: }) {
00691:   const ufTrim = String(extractUfParam(uf) || "").trim();
00692:   const ufUp = ufTrim.toUpperCase();
00693: 
00694:   if (isFederalInput(ufUp)) {
00695:     for (const lk of FEDERAL_LOTTERY_KEYS) {
00696:       const { snap, error } = await queryDrawsByField({
00697:         fieldName: "lottery_key",
00698:         uf: lk,
00699:         extraWheres,
00700:         extraOrderBy,
00701:         extraLimit,
00702:         policy,
00703:       });
00704: 
00705:       if (error) return { docs: [], usedField: "lottery_key", error };
00706:       if (snap?.docs?.length) {
00707:         return { docs: snap.docs, usedField: "lottery_key", error: null };
00708:       }
00709:     }
00710: 
00711:     return { docs: [], usedField: "lottery_key", error: null };
00712:   }
00713: 
00714:   /*
00715:    * RJ possui documentos históricos gravados em dois formatos:
00716:    * - uf == "RJ"
00717:    * - lottery_key == "PT_RIO"
00718:    *
00719:    * Não podemos retornar somente a primeira consulta que encontrar dados,
00720:    * pois isso elimina horários existentes apenas no outro formato.
00721:    */
00722:   if (ufUp === RJ_STATE_CODE || ufUp === RJ_LOTTERY_KEY) {
00723:     const [byUf, byLotteryKey] = await Promise.all([
00724:       queryDrawsByField({
00725:         fieldName: "uf",
00726:         uf: RJ_STATE_CODE,
00727:         extraWheres,
00728:         extraOrderBy,
00729:         extraLimit,
00730:         policy,
00731:       }),
00732:       queryDrawsByField({
00733:         fieldName: "lottery_key",
00734:         uf: RJ_LOTTERY_KEY,
00735:         extraWheres,
00736:         extraOrderBy,
00737:         extraLimit,
00738:         policy,
00739:       }),
00740:     ]);
00741: 
00742:     if (byUf.error) {
00743:       return { docs: [], usedField: "uf+lottery_key", error: byUf.error };
00744:     }
00745: 
00746:     if (byLotteryKey.error) {
00747:       return {
00748:         docs: [],
00749:         usedField: "uf+lottery_key",
00750:         error: byLotteryKey.error,
00751:       };
00752:     }
00753: 
00754:     const mergedById = new Map();
00755: 
00756:     for (const doc of byUf.snap?.docs || []) {
00757:       mergedById.set(doc.id, doc);
00758:     }
00759: 
00760:     for (const doc of byLotteryKey.snap?.docs || []) {
00761:       mergedById.set(doc.id, doc);
00762:     }
00763: 
00764:     return {
00765:       docs: Array.from(mergedById.values()),
00766:       usedField: "uf+lottery_key",
00767:       error: null,
00768:     };
00769:   }
00770: 
00771:   {
00772:     const { snap, error } = await queryDrawsByField({
00773:       fieldName: "uf",
00774:       uf: ufUp,
00775:       extraWheres,
00776:       extraOrderBy,
00777:       extraLimit,
00778:       policy,
00779:     });
00780: 
00781:     if (!error && snap?.docs?.length) {
00782:       return { docs: snap.docs, usedField: "uf", error: null };
00783:     }
00784:     if (error) return { docs: [], usedField: "uf", error };
00785:   }
00786: 
00787:   {
00788:     const lotteryKey = resolveLotteryKeyForQuery(ufTrim);
00789: 
00790:     const { snap, error } = await queryDrawsByField({
00791:       fieldName: "lottery_key",
00792:       uf: String(lotteryKey).toUpperCase(),
00793:       extraWheres,
00794:       extraOrderBy,
00795:       extraLimit,
00796:       policy,
00797:     });
00798: 
00799:     if (!error && snap?.docs?.length) {
00800:       return { docs: snap.docs, usedField: "lottery_key", error: null };
00801:     }
00802:     if (error) return { docs: [], usedField: "lottery_key", error };
00803:   }
00804: 
00805:   return { docs: [], usedField: "none", error: null };
00806: }
00807: 
00808: /* =========================
00809:    Bounds (min/max reais)
00810: ========================= */
00811: 
00812: function pickMinMaxFromMapped(mapped) {
00813:   let minYmd = null;
00814:   let maxYmd = null;
00815: 
00816:   for (const d of mapped) {
00817:     const y = d?.ymd || normalizeToYMD(d?.date);
00818:     if (!y || !isYMD(y)) continue;
00819:     if (!minYmd || y < minYmd) minYmd = y;
00820:     if (!maxYmd || y > maxYmd) maxYmd = y;
00821:   }
00822: 
00823:   return { minYmd, maxYmd };
00824: }
00825: 
00826: /**
00827:  * ✅ “Prova de vida” do maxYmd sem depender de índice composto nem de docId:
00828:  * tenta dias recentes (==) desc até achar pelo menos 1 draw.
00829:  */
00830: async function probeRecentMaxYmd(uf, lookbackDays = 45) {
00831:   const base = utcTodayYmd();
00832:   const n = Math.max(3, Math.min(120, Number(lookbackDays) || 45));
00833: 
00834:   for (let i = 0; i <= n; i += 1) {
00835:     const day = addDaysUTCLocal(base, -i);
00836:     const { docs, error } = await fetchDrawDocsPreferUf({
00837:       uf,
00838:       extraWheres: [where("ymd", "==", day)],
00839:       policy: "server",
00840:       extraLimit: limit(5),
00841:     });
00842:     if (error) return { ok: false, maxYmd: null, error };
00843:     if (docs && docs.length) return { ok: true, maxYmd: day, error: null };
00844:   }
00845: 
00846:   return { ok: false, maxYmd: null, error: null };
00847: }
00848: 
00849: /**
00850:  * ✅ API bounds (backend) — fonte única preferencial
00851:  */
00852: async function fetchBoundsFromApi(ufOrObj) {
00853:   const uf = String(extractUfParam(ufOrObj) || "").trim();
00854:   if (!uf) return { ok: false, error: new Error("uf vazio") };
00855: 
00856:   const lot = canonicalScopeKey(uf) || String(uf).trim().toUpperCase();
00857:   const url = apiUrl(`/api/bounds?lottery=${encodeURIComponent(lot)}`);
00858:   try {
00859:     const r = await fetch(url, { method: "GET" });
00860:     const j = await r.json().catch(() => null);
00861: 
00862:     if (!r.ok || !j || !j.ok) {
00863:       return {
00864:         ok: false,
00865:         error: new Error(`bounds api not ok: ${j?.message || r.status}`),
00866:         payload: j || null,
00867:       };
00868:     }
00869: 
00870:     const minYmd = String(j.minYmd || "").trim();
00871:     const maxYmd = String(j.maxYmd || "").trim();
00872:     if (!isYMD(minYmd) || !isYMD(maxYmd)) {
00873:       return {
00874:         ok: false,
00875:         error: new Error("bounds api retornou min/max inválidos"),
00876:         payload: j,
00877:       };
00878:     }
00879: 
00880:     return {
00881:       ok: true,
00882:       uf,
00883:       lottery: String(j.lottery || lot).trim(),
00884:       minYmd,
00885:       maxYmd,
00886:       minDocId: j.minDocId || null,
00887:       maxDocId: j.maxDocId || null,
00888:       source: String(j.source || "api_bounds"),
00889:     };
00890:   } catch (e) {
00891:     return { ok: false, error: e };
00892:   }
00893: }
00894: 
00895: export async function getKingBoundsByUf(ufOrObj) {
00896:   const uf = String(extractUfParam(ufOrObj) || "").trim();
00897:   if (!uf) throw new Error("Parâmetro obrigatório: uf");
00898: 
00899:   const apiTry = await fetchBoundsFromApi(ufOrObj);
00900:   if (apiTry?.ok) {
00901:     const scopeKey = canonicalScopeKey(uf);
00902:     const floored = applyBoundsFloor(scopeKey, {
00903:       minYmd: apiTry.minYmd,
00904:       maxYmd: apiTry.maxYmd,
00905:     });
00906: 
00907:     return {
00908:       ok: true,
00909:       uf,
00910: 
00911:       minYmd: floored.minYmd,
00912:       maxYmd: floored.maxYmd,
00913: 
00914:       minDate: floored.minYmd,
00915:       maxDate: floored.maxYmd,
00916: 
00917:       minDocId: apiTry.minDocId || null,
00918:       maxDocId: apiTry.maxDocId || null,
00919: 
00920:       source: `api_bounds:${apiTry.source}`,
00921:     };
00922:   }
00923: 
00924:   const SCAN_LIMIT = 50;
00925:   const FALLBACK_EDGE_LIMIT = 600;
00926: 
00927:   let firstTryError = null;
00928: 
00929:   const DOC_ID = documentId();
00930: 
00931:   async function sampleEdgesByDocId(fieldName, ufValue) {
00932:     const asc = await queryDrawsByField({
00933:       fieldName,
00934:       uf: ufValue,
00935:       extraOrderBy: [orderBy(DOC_ID)],
00936:       extraLimit: limit(FALLBACK_EDGE_LIMIT),
00937:       policy: "server",
00938:     });
00939: 
00940:     const last = await queryDrawsByField({
00941:       fieldName,
00942:       uf: ufValue,
00943:       extraOrderBy: [orderBy(DOC_ID)],
00944:       extraLimit: limitToLast(FALLBACK_EDGE_LIMIT),
00945:       policy: "server",
00946:     });
00947: 
00948:     const mappedA = (asc?.snap?.docs || []).map(mapDrawDoc);
00949:     const mappedB = (last?.snap?.docs || []).map(mapDrawDoc);
00950: 
00951:     const merged = dedupeDrawsLocal([...mappedA, ...mappedB]);
00952: 
00953:     return {
00954:       merged,
00955:       ok: merged.length > 0,
00956:       errors: [asc?.error, last?.error].filter(Boolean),
00957:       sources: [asc?.source, last?.source].filter(Boolean),
00958:     };
00959:   }
00960: 
00961:   {
00962:     const orderAsc = [orderBy("ymd", "asc"), orderBy(DOC_ID)];
00963:     const orderDesc = [orderBy("ymd", "desc"), orderBy(DOC_ID)];
00964: 
00965:     const {
00966:       docs: minDocs,
00967:       usedField: usedMin,
00968:       error: eMin,
00969:     } = await fetchDrawDocsPreferUf({
00970:       uf,
00971:       extraOrderBy: orderAsc,
00972:       extraLimit: limit(SCAN_LIMIT),
00973:       policy: "server",
00974:     });
00975: 
00976:     const {
00977:       docs: maxDocs,
00978:       usedField: usedMax,
00979:       error: eMax,
00980:     } = await fetchDrawDocsPreferUf({
00981:       uf,
00982:       extraOrderBy: orderDesc,
00983:       extraLimit: limit(SCAN_LIMIT),
00984:       policy: "server",
00985:     });
00986: 
00987:     if (eMin || eMax) {
00988:       if (!isIndexError(eMin) && !isIndexError(eMax)) firstTryError = eMin || eMax;
00989:     }
00990: 
00991:     if (minDocs.length && maxDocs.length && !eMin && !eMax) {
00992:       const mappedMin = minDocs.map(mapDrawDoc);
00993:       const mappedMax = maxDocs.map(mapDrawDoc);
00994: 
00995:       const { minYmd } = pickMinMaxFromMapped(mappedMin);
00996:       const { maxYmd } = pickMinMaxFromMapped(mappedMax);
00997: 
00998:       if (minYmd && maxYmd) {
00999:         return {
01000:           ok: true,
01001:           uf,
01002:           minYmd,
01003:           maxYmd,
01004:           minDate: minYmd,
01005:           maxDate: maxYmd,
01006:           source: `ymd_scan${SCAN_LIMIT}:${usedMin}/${usedMax}`,
01007:         };
01008:       }
01009:     }
01010:   }
01011: 
01012:   const recentProbe = await probeRecentMaxYmd(uf, 60);
01013:   const recentMaxYmd = recentProbe?.ok ? recentProbe.maxYmd : null;
01014: 
01015:   const scopeKeyBounds = canonicalScopeKey(uf);
01016:   {
01017:     const idxInfo = (() => {
01018:       const code = String(firstTryError?.code || "");
01019:       const msg = String(firstTryError?.message || "");
01020:       if (!code && !msg) return "";
01021:       return `:firstTryError=${code || "err"}`;
01022:     })();
01023: 
01024:     const a = await sampleEdgesByDocId("uf", String(uf).toUpperCase());
01025: 
01026:     const ufUp = String(uf || "").trim().toUpperCase();
01027: 
01028:     if (!a.ok && isFederalInput(ufUp)) {
01029:       for (const lk of FEDERAL_LOTTERY_KEYS) {
01030:         const b = await sampleEdgesByDocId("lottery_key", lk);
01031:         if (b.ok) {
01032:           const mm = pickMinMaxFromMapped(b.merged);
01033: 
01034:           const maxFixed =
01035:             recentMaxYmd && (!mm.maxYmd || recentMaxYmd > mm.maxYmd)
01036:               ? recentMaxYmd
01037:               : mm.maxYmd;
01038: 
01039:           const floored = applyBoundsFloor(scopeKeyBounds, {
01040:             minYmd: mm.minYmd || null,
01041:             maxYmd: maxFixed || null,
01042:           });
01043: 
01044:           return {
01045:             ok: !!(floored.minYmd && floored.maxYmd),
01046:             uf,
01047: 
01048:             minYmd: floored.minYmd,
01049:             maxYmd: floored.maxYmd,
01050: 
01051:             minDate: floored.minYmd,
01052:             maxDate: floored.maxYmd,
01053: 
01054:             source: `fallback_edges_docId_limit=${FALLBACK_EDGE_LIMIT}:lottery_key(${lk}):sampleCount=${b.merged.length}${
01055:               recentMaxYmd ? ":max_probe" : ""
01056:             }${idxInfo}`,
01057:           };
01058:         }
01059:       }
01060:     }
01061: 
01062:     const lotteryKey =
01063:       ufUp === RJ_STATE_CODE ? RJ_LOTTERY_KEY : resolveLotteryKeyForQuery(uf);
01064: 
01065:     const b = a.ok
01066:       ? null
01067:       : await sampleEdgesByDocId("lottery_key", String(lotteryKey).toUpperCase());
01068: 
01069:     const merged = a.ok ? a.merged : b?.merged || [];
01070:     const mm = pickMinMaxFromMapped(merged);
01071: 
01072:     const maxFixed =
01073:       recentMaxYmd && (!mm.maxYmd || recentMaxYmd > mm.maxYmd)
01074:         ? recentMaxYmd
01075:         : mm.maxYmd;
01076: 
01077:     const floored = applyBoundsFloor(scopeKeyBounds, {
01078:       minYmd: mm.minYmd || null,
01079:       maxYmd: maxFixed || null,
01080:     });
01081: 
01082:     return {
01083:       ok: !!(floored.minYmd && floored.maxYmd),
01084:       uf,
01085: 
01086:       minYmd: floored.minYmd,
01087:       maxYmd: floored.maxYmd,
01088: 
01089:       minDate: floored.minYmd,
01090:       maxDate: floored.maxYmd,
01091: 
01092:       source: `fallback_edges_docId_limit=${FALLBACK_EDGE_LIMIT}:${
01093:         a.ok ? "uf" : "lottery_key"
01094:       }:sampleCount=${merged.length}${recentMaxYmd ? ":max_probe" : ""}${idxInfo}`,
01095:     };
01096:   }
01097: }
01098: 
01099: /* =========================
01100:    API: Day
01101: ========================= */
01102: 
01103: function buildPrizeSignature(draw) {
01104:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
01105:   if (!prizes.length) return "";
01106: 
01107:   const parts = prizes
01108:     .map((p) => {
01109:       const pos = Number(p?.position);
01110:       const grupo = Number(p?.grupo);
01111:       const numero = toPrizeDigitsByPosition(
01112:         p?.numero ?? p?.milhar ?? p?.number ?? p?.num ?? p?.valor ?? "",
01113:         pos
01114:       );
01115: 
01116:       if (!Number.isFinite(pos) || !Number.isFinite(grupo) || !numero) return "";
01117:       return `${pad2(pos)}:${pad2(grupo)}:${numero}`;
01118:     })
01119:     .filter(Boolean)
01120:     .sort();
01121: 
01122:   return parts.join("|");
01123: }
01124: 
01125: function federalHourPriority(draw) {
01126:   const h = normalizeHourLike(
01127:     draw?.close_hour || draw?.closeHour || draw?.hour || draw?.hora || ""
01128:   );
01129:   const ymd = String(draw?.ymd || normalizeToYMD(draw?.date) || "").trim();
01130:   const n = hourToNumSafe(h);
01131: 
01132:   const prefer20h = getFederalPreferredHourForYmd(ymd) === "20:00";
01133: 
01134:   if (prefer20h) {
01135:     if (h === "20:00") return 200000 + n;
01136:     if (h === "19:00") return 190000 + n;
01137:     return 100000 + n;
01138:   }
01139: 
01140:   if (h === "19:00") return 200000 + n;
01141:   if (h === "20:00") return 190000 + n;
01142:   return 100000 + n;
01143: }
01144: 
01145: function pickBetterFederalClone(a, b) {
01146:   const pa = Number.isFinite(Number(a?.prizesCount))
01147:     ? Number(a.prizesCount)
01148:     : Array.isArray(a?.prizes)
01149:     ? a.prizes.length
01150:     : 0;
01151: 
01152:   const pb = Number.isFinite(Number(b?.prizesCount))
01153:     ? Number(b.prizesCount)
01154:     : Array.isArray(b?.prizes)
01155:     ? b.prizes.length
01156:     : 0;
01157: 
01158:   if (pa !== pb) return pb > pa ? b : a;
01159: 
01160:   const ha = federalHourPriority(a);
01161:   const hb = federalHourPriority(b);
01162:   if (ha !== hb) return hb > ha ? b : a;
01163: 
01164:   const ia = String(a?.drawId || a?.id || "");
01165:   const ib = String(b?.drawId || b?.id || "");
01166:   return ib.localeCompare(ia) > 0 ? b : a;
01167: }
01168: 
01169: function collapseFederalDailyCloneDraws(draws) {
01170:   const arr = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
01171:   const byKey = new Map();
01172: 
01173:   for (const d of arr) {
01174:     const ymd = String(d?.ymd || normalizeToYMD(d?.date) || "").trim();
01175:     const sig = buildPrizeSignature(d);
01176: 
01177:     if (!ymd || !sig) {
01178:       const fallbackKey = `id::${String(
01179:         d?.drawId || d?.id || Math.random()
01180:       )}`;
01181:       if (!byKey.has(fallbackKey)) byKey.set(fallbackKey, d);
01182:       continue;
01183:     }
01184: 
01185:     const key = `${ymd}__${sig}`;
01186:     const prev = byKey.get(key);
01187: 
01188:     if (!prev) byKey.set(key, d);
01189:     else byKey.set(key, pickBetterFederalClone(prev, d));
01190:   }
01191: 
01192:   return sortDrawsLocal(Array.from(byKey.values()));
01193: }
01194: 
01195: function drawsCacheKeyDay({ scopeKey, ymd, positionsArr, hourFilter }) {
01196:   const p = positionsArr && positionsArr.length ? positionsArr.join(",") : "all";
01197:   const h =
01198:     hourFilter?.kind === "bucket"
01199:       ? `bucket:${hourFilter.bucket}`
01200:       : hourFilter?.kind === "exact"
01201:       ? `exact:${hourFilter.hhmm}`
01202:       : "all";
01203:   return `day::${scopeKey}::${ymd}::pos=${p}::h=${h}`;
01204: }
01205: 
01206: export async function getKingResultsByDate({
01207:   uf,
01208:   date,
01209:   closeHour = null,
01210:   closeHourBucket = null,
01211:   positions = null,
01212: }) {
01213:   if (!uf || !date) throw new Error("Parâmetros obrigatórios: uf e date");
01214: 
01215:   const scopeKey = canonicalScopeKey(uf);
01216: 
01217:   const positionsArr = normalizePositions(positions);
01218:   const ymdDate = normalizeToYMD(date);
01219:   if (!ymdDate) return [];
01220: 
01221:   const hourFilter = resolveHourFilter({ closeHour, closeHourBucket });
01222: 
01223:   const dayKey = drawsCacheKeyDay({
01224:     scopeKey,
01225:     ymd: ymdDate,
01226:     positionsArr,
01227:     hourFilter,
01228:   });
01229: 
01230:   const forceFreshServer = shouldForceFreshServerRead(ymdDate);
01231:   const allowMemoryCache = !forceFreshServer;
01232: 
01233:   if (allowMemoryCache) {
01234:     const cached = cacheGet(DRAWS_CACHE, dayKey);
01235:     if (cached) return cached;
01236:   }
01237: 
01238:   const readPolicy = forceFreshServer ? "server" : DEFAULT_READ_POLICY;
01239: 
01240:   const docCandidates = [];
01241: 
01242:   {
01243:     const { docs: found, error } = await fetchDrawDocsPreferUf({
01244:       uf,
01245:       extraWheres: [where("ymd", "==", ymdDate)],
01246:       policy: readPolicy,
01247:     });
01248:     if (error) throw error;
01249:     if (found.length) docCandidates.push(...found);
01250:   }
01251: 
01252:   if (!docCandidates.length) {
01253:     const rawDate = String(date || "").trim();
01254:     if (rawDate) {
01255:       const { docs: found, error } = await fetchDrawDocsPreferUf({
01256:         uf,
01257:         extraWheres: [where("date", "==", rawDate)],
01258:         policy: readPolicy,
01259:       });
01260:       if (error) throw error;
01261:       if (found.length) docCandidates.push(...found);
01262:     }
01263:   }
01264: 
01265:   if (!docCandidates.length) {
01266:     const { docs: found, error } = await fetchDrawDocsPreferUf({
01267:       uf,
01268:       extraWheres: [where("date", "==", ymdDate)],
01269:       policy: readPolicy,
01270:     });
01271:     if (error) throw error;
01272:     if (found.length) docCandidates.push(...found);
01273:   }
01274: 
01275:   if (!docCandidates.length) {
01276:     const brDate = ymdToBR(ymdDate);
01277:     if (brDate) {
01278:       const { docs: found, error } = await fetchDrawDocsPreferUf({
01279:         uf,
01280:         extraWheres: [where("date", "==", brDate)],
01281:         policy: readPolicy,
01282:       });
01283:       if (error) throw error;
01284:       if (found.length) docCandidates.push(...found);
01285:     }
01286:   }
01287: 
01288:   if (!docCandidates.length) return [];
01289: 
01290:   let baseAll = dedupeDrawsLocal(docCandidates.map(mapDrawDoc));
01291:   baseAll = baseAll.filter((x) => (x.ymd || normalizeToYMD(x.date)) === ymdDate);
01292: 
01293:   const orderedAll = sortDrawsLocal(baseAll);
01294: 
01295:   const hydratedAll = await mapWithConcurrency(orderedAll, 6, async (item) => {
01296:     const prizes = await fetchPrizesForDraw(item.drawId, positionsArr, item.prizes);
01297:     const pc = Array.isArray(prizes) ? prizes.length : 0;
01298:     return { ...item, prizes, prizesCount: pc, __mode: "detailed" };
01299:   });
01300: 
01301:   let dayAll = dedupeDrawsLocal(hydratedAll);
01302: 
01303: if (scopeKey === FEDERAL_SCOPE_CODE) {
01304:   dayAll = collapseFederalDailyCloneDraws(dayAll);
01305: }
01306: 
01307:   const base = hourFilter?.kind
01308:     ? dayAll.filter((d) => drawPassesHourFilter(d, hourFilter))
01309:     : dayAll;
01310: 
01311:   let out = dedupeDrawsLocal(sortDrawsLocal(base));
01312: 
01313: if (scopeKey === FEDERAL_SCOPE_CODE) {
01314:   out = collapseFederalDailyCloneDraws(out);
01315: }
01316: 
01317:   if (allowMemoryCache) {
01318:     cacheSet(DRAWS_CACHE, dayKey, out);
01319:   }
01320: 
01321:   return out;
01322: }
01323: 
01324: /* =========================
01325:    API: Range
01326: ========================= */
01327: 
01328: function drawsCacheKeyRange({
01329:   scopeKey,
01330:   from,
01331:   to,
01332:   positionsArr,
01333:   hourFilter,
01334:   mode,
01335: }) {
01336:   const p = positionsArr && positionsArr.length ? positionsArr.join(",") : "all";
01337:   const h =
01338:     hourFilter?.kind === "bucket"
01339:       ? `bucket:${hourFilter.bucket}`
01340:       : hourFilter?.kind === "exact"
01341:       ? `exact:${hourFilter.hhmm}`
01342:       : "all";
01343:   const m = mode ? String(mode) : "detailed";
01344:   return `range::${scopeKey}::${from}..${to}::pos=${p}::h=${h}::mode=${m}`;
01345: }
01346: 
01347: function chooseRangeConcurrency(drawCount) {
01348:   if (!Number.isFinite(drawCount)) return 6;
01349:   if (drawCount <= 50) return 4;
01350:   if (drawCount <= 300) return 6;
01351:   if (drawCount <= 1200) return 8;
01352:   return 10;
01353: }
01354: 
01355: export function decideKingRangeMode({ dateFrom, dateTo, mode = "detailed" }) {
01356:   const ymdFrom = normalizeToYMD(dateFrom);
01357:   const ymdTo = normalizeToYMD(dateTo);
01358:   if (!ymdFrom || !ymdTo) return "detailed";
01359: 
01360:   if (mode === "detailed" || mode === "aggregated") return mode;
01361:   if (mode !== "auto") return "detailed";
01362: 
01363:   const days = daysBetweenInclusiveUTC(ymdFrom, ymdTo);
01364:   if (!Number.isFinite(days) || days <= 0) return "detailed";
01365:   return "detailed";
01366: }

01368: /**
01369:  * ✅ Fallback barato (SEM prizes): usado quando falta índice composto no RANGE,
01370:  * ou quando você quer aggregated sem custo de hidratar.
01371:  */
01372: async function fetchDrawsDayNoPrizes({ uf, dayYmd }) {
01373:   const readPolicy = shouldForceFreshServerRead(dayYmd) ? "server" : DEFAULT_READ_POLICY;
01374: 
01375:   const { docs, error } = await fetchDrawDocsPreferUf({
01376:     uf,
01377:     extraWheres: [where("ymd", "==", dayYmd)],
01378:     policy: readPolicy,
01379:   });
01380:   if (error) throw error;
01381:   if (!docs || !docs.length) return [];
01382: 
01383:   let baseAll = dedupeDrawsLocal(docs.map(mapDrawDoc));
01384:   baseAll = baseAll.filter((x) => (x.ymd || normalizeToYMD(x.date)) === dayYmd);
01385: 
01386:   const ordered = sortDrawsLocal(baseAll);
01387: 
01388:   return ordered.map((d) => {
01389:     const embedded = normalizeEmbeddedPrizesForAggregated(d?.prizes);
01390:     return {
01391:       ...d,
01392:       prizes: embedded,
01393:       prizesCount: embedded.length || Number(d?.prizesCount || 0),
01394:       __mode: "aggregated",
01395:     };
01396:   });
01397: }

01399: export async function getKingResultsByRange({
01400:   uf,
01401:   dateFrom,
01402:   dateTo,
01403:   closeHour = null,
01404:   closeHourBucket = null,
01405:   positions = null,
01406:   mode = "detailed",
01407:   readPolicy = DEFAULT_READ_POLICY,
01408:   bypassCache = false,
01409: }) {
01410:   if (!uf || !dateFrom || !dateTo) {
01411:     throw new Error("Parâmetros obrigatórios: uf, dateFrom, dateTo");
01412:   }
01413: 
01414:   const scopeKey = canonicalScopeKey(uf);
01415: 
01416:   const positionsArr = normalizePositions(positions);
01417: 
01418:   const ymdFrom = normalizeToYMD(dateFrom);
01419:   const ymdTo = normalizeToYMD(dateTo);
01420:   if (!ymdFrom || !ymdTo) return [];
01421: 
01422:   const hourFilter = resolveHourFilter({ closeHour, closeHourBucket });
01423: 
01424:   const effectiveMode = decideKingRangeMode({
01425:     dateFrom: ymdFrom,
01426:     dateTo: ymdTo,
01427:     mode,
01428:   });
01429: 
01430:   const rangeKey = drawsCacheKeyRange({
01431:     scopeKey,
01432:     from: ymdFrom,
01433:     to: ymdTo,
01434:     positionsArr,
01435:     hourFilter,
01436:     mode: effectiveMode,
01437:   });
01438:   if (!bypassCache) {
01439:     const cached = cacheGet(DRAWS_CACHE, rangeKey);
01440:     if (cached) return cached;
01441:   }
01442: 
01443:   const DOC_ID = documentId();
01444:   const rangeOrder = [orderBy("ymd", "asc"), orderBy(DOC_ID)];
01445: 
01446:   const { docs, error, usedField } = await fetchDrawDocsPreferUf({
01447:     uf,
01448:     extraWheres: [where("ymd", ">=", ymdFrom), where("ymd", "<=", ymdTo)],
01449:     extraOrderBy: rangeOrder,
01450:     policy: readPolicy,
01451:   });
01452: 
01453:   if (error) {
01454:     if (isIndexError(error)) {
01455:       const days = daysBetweenInclusiveUTC(ymdFrom, ymdTo);
01456:       const MAX_FALLBACK_DAYS = 31;
01457: 
01458:       if (Number.isFinite(days) && days > 0 && days <= MAX_FALLBACK_DAYS) {
01459:         if (effectiveMode === "aggregated") {
01460:           const all = [];
01461:           for (let i = 0; i < days; i += 1) {
01462:             const day = addDaysUTC(ymdFrom, i);
01463:             const dayDraws = await fetchDrawsDayNoPrizes({ uf, dayYmd: day });
01464:             if (dayDraws.length) all.push(...dayDraws);
01465:           }
01466: 
01467:           const baseAll = dedupeDrawsLocal(all);
01468: 
01469: const base = hourFilter?.kind
01470:             ? baseAll.filter((d) => drawPassesHourFilter(d, hourFilter))
01471:             : baseAll;
01472: 
01473:           let out = dedupeDrawsLocal(sortDrawsLocal(base));
01474: 
01475:           if (!bypassCache) {
01476:       cacheSet(DRAWS_CACHE, rangeKey, out);
01477:     }
01478:           return out;
01479:         }
01480: 
01481:         const all = [];
01482:         for (let i = 0; i < days; i += 1) {
01483:           const day = addDaysUTC(ymdFrom, i);
01484:           const dayDraws = await getKingResultsByDate({
01485:             uf,
01486:             date: day,
01487:             closeHour,
01488:             closeHourBucket,
01489:             positions: positionsArr,
01490:           });
01491:           if (Array.isArray(dayDraws) && dayDraws.length) all.push(...dayDraws);
01492:         }
01493: 
01494:         const baseAll = dedupeDrawsLocal(all);
01495:         const base = hourFilter?.kind
01496:           ? baseAll.filter((d) => drawPassesHourFilter(d, hourFilter))
01497:           : baseAll;
01498: 
01499:         let out = dedupeDrawsLocal(sortDrawsLocal(base));
01500: 
01501:         if (!bypassCache) {
01502:       cacheSet(DRAWS_CACHE, rangeKey, out);
01503:     }
01504:         return out;
01505:       }
01506: 
01507:       const rawCode = String(error?.code || "");
01508:       const rawMsg = String(error?.message || "");
01509: 
01510:       throw new Error(
01511:         `Firestore: falta índice composto para RANGE (campo ${usedField} + ymd).\n` +
01512:           `Crie/valide o índice no console (Collection: draws | Fields: ${usedField} ASC, ymd ASC, __name__ (documentId) ASC).\n` +
01513:           `Obs: fallback automático só é aplicado para ranges até ${MAX_FALLBACK_DAYS} dias.\n` +
01514:           (rawCode || rawMsg ? `Detalhe: ${rawCode} ${rawMsg}` : "")
01515:       );
01516:     }
01517: 
01518:     throw error;
01519:   }
01520: 
01521:   if (!docs || !docs.length) {
01522:     const days = daysBetweenInclusiveUTC(ymdFrom, ymdTo);
01523:     const MAX_EMPTY_RANGE_FALLBACK_DAYS = 120;
01524: 
01525:     if (
01526:       Number.isFinite(days) &&
01527:       days > 0 &&
01528:       days <= MAX_EMPTY_RANGE_FALLBACK_DAYS
01529:     ) {
01530:       if (effectiveMode === "aggregated") {
01531:         const all = [];
01532:         for (let i = 0; i < days; i += 1) {
01533:           const day = addDaysUTC(ymdFrom, i);
01534:           const dayDraws = await fetchDrawsDayNoPrizes({ uf, dayYmd: day });
01535:           if (dayDraws.length) all.push(...dayDraws);
01536:         }
01537: 
01538:         const baseAll = dedupeDrawsLocal(all);
01539:         const base = hourFilter?.kind
01540:           ? baseAll.filter((d) => drawPassesHourFilter(d, hourFilter))
01541:           : baseAll;
01542: 
01543:         let out = dedupeDrawsLocal(sortDrawsLocal(base));
01544: 
01545:         if (!bypassCache) {
01546:       cacheSet(DRAWS_CACHE, rangeKey, out);
01547:     }
01548:         return out;
01549:       }
01550: 
01551:       const all = [];
01552:       for (let i = 0; i < days; i += 1) {
01553:         const day = addDaysUTC(ymdFrom, i);
01554:         const dayDraws = await getKingResultsByDate({
01555:           uf,
01556:           date: day,
01557:           closeHour,
01558:           closeHourBucket,
01559:           positions: positionsArr,
01560:         });
01561:         if (Array.isArray(dayDraws) && dayDraws.length) all.push(...dayDraws);
01562:       }
01563: 
01564:       const baseAll = dedupeDrawsLocal(all);
01565:       const base = hourFilter?.kind
01566:         ? baseAll.filter((d) => drawPassesHourFilter(d, hourFilter))
01567:         : baseAll;
01568: 
01569:       const ordered = sortDrawsLocal(base);
01570: 
01571:       const conc = chooseRangeConcurrency(ordered.length);
01572:       const results = await mapWithConcurrency(ordered, conc, async (item) => {
01573:         const prizes = await fetchPrizesForDraw(item.drawId, positionsArr, item.prizes);
01574:         const pc = Array.isArray(prizes) ? prizes.length : 0;
01575:         return { ...item, prizes, prizesCount: pc, __mode: "detailed" };
01576:       });
01577: 
01578:       const out = dedupeDrawsLocal(results);
01579:       if (!bypassCache) {
01580:       cacheSet(DRAWS_CACHE, rangeKey, out);
01581:     }
01582:       return out;
01583:     }
01584: 
01585:     return [];
01586:   }
01587: 
01588:   const baseAll = dedupeDrawsLocal(docs.map(mapDrawDoc));
01589:   const base = hourFilter?.kind
01590:     ? baseAll.filter((d) => drawPassesHourFilter(d, hourFilter))
01591:     : baseAll;
01592: 
01593:   const ordered = sortDrawsLocal(base);
01594: 
01595:   if (effectiveMode === "aggregated") {
01596:     const out = dedupeDrawsLocal(
01597:       ordered.map((d) => {
01598:         const embedded = normalizeEmbeddedPrizesForAggregated(d?.prizes);
01599:         return {
01600:           ...d,
01601:           prizes: embedded,
01602:           prizesCount: Number.isFinite(Number(d?.prizesCount))
01603:             ? Number(d.prizesCount)
01604:             : embedded.length || 0,
01605:           __mode: "aggregated",
01606:         };
01607:       })
01608:     );
01609:     if (!bypassCache) {
01610:       cacheSet(DRAWS_CACHE, rangeKey, out);
01611:     }
01612:     return out;
01613:   }
01614: 
01615:   const conc = chooseRangeConcurrency(ordered.length);
01616: 
01617:   const results = await mapWithConcurrency(ordered, conc, async (item) => {
01618:     const prizes = await fetchPrizesForDraw(item.drawId, positionsArr, item.prizes);
01619:     const pc = Array.isArray(prizes) ? prizes.length : 0;
01620:     return { ...item, prizes, prizesCount: pc, __mode: "detailed" };
01621:   });
01622: 
01623:   const out = dedupeDrawsLocal(results);
01624:   if (!bypassCache) {
01625:       cacheSet(DRAWS_CACHE, rangeKey, out);
01626:     }
01627:   return out;
01628: }
01629: 
01630: /* =========================
01631:    ✅ Hidratação de prizes (para UX no "Todos")
01632: ========================= */
01633: 
01634: export async function hydrateKingDrawsWithPrizes({
01635:   draws,
01636:   positions = null,
01637:   concurrency = null,
01638: }) {
01639:   const arr = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
01640:   if (!arr.length) return [];
01641: 
01642:   const positionsArr = normalizePositions(positions);
01643: 
01644:   const conc =
01645:     Number.isFinite(Number(concurrency)) && Number(concurrency) > 0
01646:       ? Number(concurrency)
01647:       : chooseRangeConcurrency(arr.length);
01648: 
01649:   const results = await mapWithConcurrency(arr, conc, async (item) => {
01650:     const prizes = await fetchPrizesForDraw(item.drawId, positionsArr, item.prizes);
01651:     const pc = Array.isArray(prizes) ? prizes.length : 0;
01652:     return { ...item, prizes, prizesCount: pc, __mode: "detailed" };
01653:   });
01654: 
01655:   return dedupeDrawsLocal(results);
01656: }

01658: /* =========================
01659:    ✅ ATRASADOS (Late) — 100% baseado na SUA base
01660: ========================= */
01661: 
01662: function normalizeLotteriesList(lotteries) {
01663:   const arr = Array.isArray(lotteries) ? lotteries : [];
01664:   const cleaned = arr
01665:     .map((x) => String(x ?? "").trim().toUpperCase())
01666:     .filter(Boolean);
01667:   return cleaned.length ? Array.from(new Set(cleaned)) : null;
01668: }
01669: 
01670: function drawPassesLotteriesFilter(draw, lotteriesArr) {
01671:   if (!lotteriesArr || !lotteriesArr.length) return true;
01672: 
01673:   const identities = [
01674:     draw?.lottery_key,
01675:     draw?.lotteryKey,
01676:     draw?.lottery_code,
01677:     draw?.lotteryCode,
01678:     draw?.lot_code,
01679:     draw?.lotCode,
01680:     draw?.lot,
01681:     draw?.code,
01682:   ]
01683:     .map((value) => String(value ?? "").trim().toUpperCase())
01684:     .filter(Boolean);
01685: 
01686:   return identities.some((identity) =>
01687:     lotteriesArr.includes(identity)
01688:   );
01689: }
01690: 
01691: function lateCacheKey({
01692:   scopeKey,
01693:   fromYmd,
01694:   toYmd,
01695:   baseYmd,
01696:   prizePosition,
01697:   hourFilter,
01698:   lotteriesArr,
01699:   chunkDays,
01700: }) {
01701:   const h =
01702:     hourFilter?.kind === "bucket"
01703:       ? `bucket:${hourFilter.bucket}`
01704:       : hourFilter?.kind === "exact"
01705:       ? `exact:${hourFilter.hhmm}`
01706:       : "all";
01707:   const lots = lotteriesArr && lotteriesArr.length ? lotteriesArr.join(",") : "all";
01708:   const cd = Number(chunkDays) || 15;
01709:   const pos = Number(prizePosition) || 1;
01710:   return `late::${scopeKey}::${fromYmd}..${toYmd}::base=${baseYmd}::pos=${pos}::h=${h}::lots=${lots}::chunk=${cd}`;
01711: }
01712: 
01713: function hourToNumSafe(hhmm) {
01714:   const s = normalizeHourLike(hhmm);
01715:   const m = s.match(/^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
01716:   if (!m) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
01717:   return Number(m[1]) * 100 + Number(m[2]);
01718: }
01719: 
01720: export async function getKingLateByRange({
01721:   uf,
01722:   dateFrom,
01723:   dateTo,
01724:   baseDate = null,
01725:   lotteries = null,
01726:   prizePosition = 1,
01727:   closeHour = null,
01728:   closeHourBucket = null,
01729:   chunkDays = 15,
01730: }) {
01731:   if (!uf || !dateFrom || !dateTo) {
01732:     throw new Error("Parâmetros obrigatórios: uf, dateFrom, dateTo");
01733:   }
01734:

function drawTs(draw) {
  const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
  const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));

  if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;

  const [Y, M, D] = String(y).split("-").map(Number);
  const mins = hourToInt(h);

  if (!Number.isFinite(mins) || mins < 0) {
    return Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
  }

  return Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D) + mins * 60 * 1000;
}

function top3ItemMatchesSlot(item, slot) {
  const targetY = safeStr(slot?.targetYmd || "");
  const targetH = toHourBucket(slot?.targetHour || "");

  const nextY = safeStr(item?.meta?.next?.ymd || "");
  const nextH = toHourBucket(item?.meta?.next?.hour || "");

  if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH) return false;
  if (!isYMD(nextY) || !nextH) return false;

  return nextY === targetY && nextH === targetH;
}

function findRealDrawByTarget({ targetYmd, targetHour, todayDraws, rangeDraws }) {
  const ymd = safeStr(targetYmd);
  const hour = normHour(targetHour);

  if (!isYMD(ymd) || !hour) return null;

  const sameTarget = (d) =>
    pickDrawYMD(d) === ymd && normHour(pickDrawHour(d)) === hour;

  const realToday =
    (Array.isArray(todayDraws) ? todayDraws : []).find(sameTarget) || null;

  const realRange =
    (Array.isArray(rangeDraws) ? rangeDraws : []).find(sameTarget) || null;

  return realToday || realRange || null;
}

function reconcilePendingTop3Log({ todayDraws, rangeDraws }) {
  const log = getTop3Log();
  if (!Array.isArray(log) || !log.length) return;

  let changed = false;

  const nextLog = log.map((entry) => {
    if (!entry || entry.result != null) return entry;

    const targetYmd = safeStr(entry?.target?.ymd || "");
    const targetHour = normHour(entry?.target?.hour || "");

    if (!isYMD(targetYmd) || !targetHour) return entry;
    if (isFutureTarget(targetYmd, targetHour)) return entry;

    const real = findRealDrawByTarget({
      targetYmd,
      targetHour,
      todayDraws,
      rangeDraws,
    });

    if (!real) return entry;

    const result = normalizeResult(pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(real));
    if (result == null) return entry;

    const picks = normalizePicks(entry?.picks);

    changed = true;

    return {
      ...entry,
      picks,
      result,
      hit: picks.includes(result),
      validatedAt: Date.now(),
      status: "validated",
    };
  });

  if (changed) saveTop3Log(nextLog);
}

function ensureDayTimeline({ ymd, lotteryKey }) {
  if (!isYMD(ymd)) return;

  const schedule = getScheduleForLottery({
    lotteryKey,
    ymd,
    PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
    PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
    FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  });

  if (!Array.isArray(schedule) || !schedule.length) return;

  const log = getTop3Log();

  for (const h of schedule) {
    const hour = normHour(h);
    const targetKey = makeKey(ymd, hour);

    if (!targetKey) continue;

    const idx = log.findIndex(
      (l) => String(l?.targetKey || "") === String(targetKey)
    );

    if (idx >= 0) {
      const prev = log[idx] || {};
      const picks = normalizePicks(prev?.picks);
      const result = normalizeResult(prev?.result);
      const status =
        result != null ? "validated" : resolvePendingStatus(ymd, hour, picks);

      log[idx] = {
        ...prev,
        targetKey,
        target: { ymd, hour },
        picks,
        result,
        hit: result != null ? picks.includes(result) : prev?.hit ?? null,
        createdAt: prev?.createdAt || Date.now(),
        validatedAt: result != null ? prev?.validatedAt : undefined,
        status,
      };
    } else {
      log.push({
        targetKey,
        target: { ymd, hour },
        picks: [],
        result: null,
        hit: null,
        createdAt: Date.now(),
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        targetKey,
        target: { ymd, hour },
        picks: [],
        result: null,
        hit: null,
        createdAt: Date.now(),
        status: "empty",
      });
    }
  }

  saveTop3Log(log);
}

import {
  pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw,
  pickDrawYMD,
  pickDrawHour,
} from "./top3.engine";

import { isYMD, toHourBucket, hourToInt } from "./top3.formatters";

/*
TOP3+ — Cadeia de Bichos
Modelo de transição de grupos

- Entende targetYmd / targetHourBucket
- Encontra o draw imediatamente anterior ao alvo
- Separa transições por faixa de horário
- Evita ordenar sorteio sem hora como 00h
- Evita vazamento usando apenas histórico anterior ao alvo no modo target_aware
*/

function safeInt(v, fallback = 0) {
  const n = Number(v);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : fallback;

export function computeChainTransitions(draws) {
  const list = dedupeAndSortDraws(draws);
  const transitionsAny = new Map();
  const transitionsByEdge = new Map();

  for (let i = 0; i < list.length - 1; i += 1) {
    const fromDraw = list[i];
    const toDraw = list[i + 1];

    const fromGrupo = Number(pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(fromDraw));
    const toGrupo = Number(pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(toDraw));
    const fromHour = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(fromDraw));
    const toHour = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(toDraw));

    if (!Number.isFinite(fromGrupo) || !Number.isFinite(toGrupo)) continue;
    if (fromGrupo < 1 || fromGrupo > 25) continue;
    if (toGrupo < 1 || toGrupo > 25) continue;
    if (!fromHour || !toHour) continue;

    const anyRow = getNestedMap(transitionsAny, fromGrupo);
    anyRow.set(toGrupo, safeInt(anyRow.get(toGrupo), 0) + 1);

    const edgeKey = makeEdgeKey(fromHour, toHour);
    if (!edgeKey) continue;

    const edgeMap = getNestedMap(transitionsByEdge, edgeKey);
    const edgeRow = getNestedMap(edgeMap, fromGrupo);
    edgeRow.set(toGrupo, safeInt(edgeRow.get(toGrupo), 0) + 1);
  }

  return { transitionsAny, transitionsByEdge };
}

function getFirstGrupoFromDraw(draw) {
  const ps = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
  const p1 = ps.find((p) => guessPrizePos(p) === 1) || null;
  if (p1) {
    const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p1);

  }
  return pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(draw);
}

function getAllTop5Groups(draw) {
  const out = [];
  const ps = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
  for (const p of ps) {
    const pos = guessPrizePos(p);
    if (!Number.isFinite(Number(pos)) || Number(pos) < 1 || Number(pos) > 5) {
      continue;
    }
    const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p);
    if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) <= 0) continue;
    out.push({ pos: Number(pos), grupo: Number(g) });
  }
  return out.sort((a, b) => a.pos - b.pos);
}

function buildSceneHypothesisDistribution(sceneRanking, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
  const ranking = Array.isArray(sceneRanking) ? sceneRanking : [];
  const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);

  const freq = new Map();
  for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) freq.set(g, 0);

  let totalWeight = 0;

  for (const item of ranking) {
    const score = Math.max(0, Number(item?.score || 0));
    const nextGrupo = Number(item?.nextDraw ? pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(item.nextDraw) : null);

    if (!Number.isFinite(nextGrupo) || nextGrupo < 1 || nextGrupo > k) continue;
    if (score <= 0) continue;

    freq.set(nextGrupo, Number(freq.get(nextGrupo) || 0) + score);
    totalWeight += score;
  }

  const prob = new Map();
  for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
    prob.set(g, totalWeight > 0 ? Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / totalWeight : 0);
  }

  return {
    freq,
    prob,
    samples: ranking.length,
    totalWeight,
  };
}

function buildSceneHypothesisDistribution(sceneRanking, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {

function getFirstGrupoFromDraw(draw) {
  const ps = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
  const p1 = ps.find((p) => guessPrizePos(p) === 1) || null;
  if (p1) {
    const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p1);
    return Number.isFinite(Number(g)) ? Number(g) : null;
  }
  return pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(draw);
}

function getPrizeGroupsByPosition(draw, maxPosition = 3) {
  const limit = Math.max(1, Number(maxPosition || 3));
  const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];

  return Array.from({ length: limit }, (_, index) => {
    const position = index + 1;

    const prize =
      prizes.find(
        (item) => Number(guessPrizePos(item)) === position
      ) || null;

    if (!prize) return null;

    const grupo = Number(guessPrizeGrupo(prize));

    return Number.isFinite(grupo) &&
      grupo >= 1 &&
      grupo <= TOP3_GROUPS_K
      ? grupo
      : null;
  });

function buildSceneHypothesisDistribution(sceneRanking, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
  const ranking = Array.isArray(sceneRanking) ? sceneRanking : [];
  const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);

  const freq = new Map();
  for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) freq.set(g, 0);

  let totalWeight = 0;

  for (const item of ranking) {
    const score = Math.max(0, Number(item?.score || 0));
    const nextGrupo = Number(item?.nextDraw ? pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(item.nextDraw) : null);

    if (!Number.isFinite(nextGrupo) || nextGrupo < 1 || nextGrupo > k) continue;
    if (score <= 0) continue;

    freq.set(nextGrupo, Number(freq.get(nextGrupo) || 0) + score);
    totalWeight += score;
  }

  const prob = new Map();
  for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
    prob.set(g, totalWeight > 0 ? Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / totalWeight : 0);
  }

  return {
    freq,
    prob,
    samples: ranking.length,
    totalWeight,
  };
}

function normalizeMilhar(value) {
  const digits = String(value || "").replace(/\D/g, "");
  if (!digits) return "";
  return digits.slice(-4).padStart(4, "0");
}

    prizes[0] ||
    null
  );
}

LINHA: 269
  if (!isYMD(y)) return NaN;

  const [Y, M, D] = y.split("-").map((x) => Number(x));
  const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D));

  return dt.getUTCDay(); // 0=dom ... 3=qua ... 6=sáb
}

/* =========================

export function pickPrizeMilhar4(p) {
  const raw =
    p?.milhar ??
    p?.milhar4 ??
    p?.numero ??
    p?.number ??
    p?.mil ??
    p?.num ??
    p?.valor ??
    "";

  const digits = safeStr(raw).replace(/\D+/g, "");
  if (!digits) return null;

  const last4 = digits.slice(-4).padStart(4, "0");
  return /^\d{4}$/.test(last4) ? last4 : null;
}

export function getDezena2(milhar4) {
  const s = safeStr(milhar4);
  if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(s)) return null;
  return s.slice(2, 4);
}

function normHour(h) {
  return toHourBucket(h) || "";
}

function makeKey(ymd, hour) {
  const y = safeStr(ymd);
  const h = normHour(hour);
  return isYMD(y) && h ? `${y}_${h}` : "";
}

      validatedAt: Date.now(),
      status: "validated",
    };
  });

  if (changed) saveTop3Log(nextLog);
}

function getOfficialPodium(slot) {
  return [1, 2, 3]
    .map((position) => {
      const grupo = getPrizeGrupoByPosition(
        slot,
        position

        grupo < 1 ||
        grupo > 25

        return null;

        slot?.resultTop3Milhares
      )
        ? slot.resultTop3Milhares
        : [];

      const milhar =
        String(
          persistedMilhares[position - 1] || ""

LINHA: 137
        position

        grupo < 1 ||
        grupo > 25
      ) {

        slot?.resultTop3Milhares

        : [];

        String(
          persistedMilhares[position - 1] || ""
        ).replace(/\D/g, "") ||
        extractPrizeMilharByPosition(slot, position);

      return {
        position,
        grupo,
        milhar: milhar
          ? milhar.slice(-4).padStart(4, "0")
          : "",
      };
    })
    .filter(Boolean);
}

function hasOfficialResult(slot) {
  const grupo = getSlotResultGrupo(slot);
  if (Number.isFinite(grupo) && grupo >= 1 && grupo <= 25) return true;

  const milhar = extractResultMilhar(slot);
  return /^\d{4}$/.test(milhar);
}

// src/pages/Top3/Top3View.jsx
import React, { useMemo, useState, useCallback, useEffect } from "react";
import { getAnimalLabel, getImgFromGrupo } from "../../constants/bichoMap";

function extractResultMilhar(slot) {
  if (!slot || !Array.isArray(slot.prizes)) return "";

  const p1 = slot.prizes.find((p) => Number(p.position) === 1);
  if (!p1) return "";

  const num = String(
    p1.numero ||
      p1.milhar ||
      p1.number ||
      p1.valor ||
      ""
  ).replace(/\D/g, "");

  return num.padStart(4, "0");
}

function toPercent(score) {
  const n = Number(score);
  if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;
  const pct = n <= 1 ? n * 100 : n;
  return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, pct));
}

function hasOfficialResult(slot) {
  const grupo = getSlotResultGrupo(slot);
  if (Number.isFinite(grupo) && grupo >= 1 && grupo <= 25) return true;

  const milhar = extractResultMilhar(slot);
  return /^\d{4}$/.test(milhar);
}

import { isYMD, toHourBucket } from "../top3.formatters";

import {
  PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,

} from "../top3.engine";

function emptyAnalytics() {
  return { top: [], meta: null };
}

function safeCacheRef(ref) {
  if (!ref || typeof ref !== "object") {
    return { current: { key: "", value: emptyAnalytics() } };
  }

  if (!ref.current || typeof ref.current !== "object") {
    ref.current = { key: "", value: emptyAnalytics() };
  }

  return ref;
}
